vaiśyas tu dhanadhānyāḍhyaḥ śūdraḥ sukham avāpnuyāt
tatas te munayaḥ sarve saṃpūjya vinatāsutam
svān eva cāśramāñ jagmur babhūvuḥ śāntitatparāḥ
sthūladarśibhir ākṛṣṭo durjñeyo hy akṛtātmabhiḥ
eṣā śrutir mahārāja dharmyā dharmabhṛtāṃ vara
surāṇāṃ brahmaṇā proktā vismitānāṃ paraṃtapa
mayāpy eṣā kathā tāta kathitā mātur antike
vasubhiḥ sattvasaṃpannais tavāpy eṣā mayocyate
tad agnihotraparamā japayajñaparāyaṇāḥ
nirāśīrbandhanāḥ santaḥ prayānty akṣarasāmyatām
ārambhayajñān utsṛjya japahomaparāyaṇāḥ
dhyāyanto manasā viṣṇuṃ gacchanti paramāṃ gatim
tad eva paramo mokṣo mokṣadvāraṃ ca bhārata
yathā viniścitātmāno gacchanti paramāṃ gatim
tvayāpageya nāmāni śrutānīha jagatpateḥ
bhīṣma uvāca
surāsuragurau deve śaṃkare 'vyaktayonaye
aśakto 'haṃ guṇān vaktuṃ mahādevasya dhīmataḥ
yo hi sarvagato devo na ca sarvatra dṛśyate
brahmaviṣṇusureśānāṃ sraṣṭā yaḥ prabhur eva ca
brahmādayaḥ piśācāntā yaṃ hi devā upāsate
prakṛtīnāṃ paratvena puruṣasya ca yaḥ paraḥ
cintyate yo yogavidbhir ṛṣibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ
akṣaraṃ paramaṃ prāhur asac ca sadasac ca yaḥ
prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ caiva kṣobhayitvā svatejasā
brahmāṇam asṛjat tasmād devadevaḥ prajāpatiḥ
ko hi śakto guṇān vaktuṃ devadevasya dhīmataḥ
garbhajanmajarāyukto martyo mṛtyusamanvitaḥ
ko hi śakto bhavaṃ jñātuṃ madvidhaḥ parameśvaram
ṛte nārāyaṇāt putra śaṅkhacakragadādharāt
eṣa vidvān yaduśreṣṭho viṣṇuḥ paramadurjayaḥ
divyacakṣur mahātejā vīkṣyate yogacakṣuṣā
rudrabhaktyā tu kṛṣṇena jagad vyāptaṃ mahātmanā
taṃ prasādya tadā devaṃ badaryāṃ kila bhārata
arthāt priyataratvaṃ ca sarvalokeṣu vai tadā
prāptavān eva rājendra suvarṇākṣān maheśvarāt
pūrṇaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu taptavān eṣa mādhavaḥ
prasādya varadaṃ devaṃ carācaraguruṃ śivam
yuge yuge tu kṛṣṇena toṣito vai maheśvaraḥ
bhaktyā paramayā rājan prītaś caiva mahātmanaḥ
aiśvaryaṃ yādṛśaṃ tasya jagadyoner mahātmanaḥ
tad ayaṃ dṛṣṭavān sākṣāt putrārthe harir acyutaḥ
yasmāt parataraṃ caiva nānyaṃ paśyāmi bhārata
vyākhyātuṃ devadevasya śakto nāmāny aśeṣataḥ
eṣa śakto mahābāhur vaktuṃ bhagavato guṇān
vaiśaṃpāyana uvāca
vibhūtiṃ caiva kārtsnyena satyāṃ māheśvarīṃ nṛpa
evam uktvā tadā bhīṣmo vāsudevaṃ mahāyaśāḥ
bhavamāhātmyasaṃyuktam idam āha pitāmahaḥ
dhyānenātha japaiḥ kāryā svadharmam ucitā sadā
deve nārāyaṇe bhaktaḥ śaṃkaraṃ sādhu pūjaya
vāsudevaṃ mahātmānam idaṃ vacanam abravīt
athocatuḥ prītiyutau tadānīṃ
tapaḥsamṛddhir bhavato 'stv avighnam
prāpyānujñāṃ gurumukhād ājamīḍha
tārkṣyaṃ tadā manasā cintayitvā
kṛtvā praṇāmaṃ śirasā bhavāya
tato 'bhyagāṃ prāg udīcīṃ suramyām
tato mayā hy anusṛṣṭaḥ khagendro
vitatya pakṣau divam utpapāta
tasmin gate vainateye 'ntarikṣaṃ
girau tadā sarvakāmopapanne
puṣpaiḥ phalaiḥ susamiddhair yathāvat
sthitaḥ prahṛṣṭo bhavabhāvabhāvitaḥ
tataḥ kṛtasvastyayanas tayāhaṃ
nivedya mātuś ca pituḥ samīpe
tathābhyanujñāya gurūṃś ca sarvān
mahābalaṃ tārkṣyam acintayaṃ vai
so 'haṃ himavataḥ pārśvaṃ prāpya tārkṣyaṃ vyasarjayam
nityapuṣpaphalopetair vṛkṣair aviralair vṛtam
supuṣpaiś ca latāgulmair nirbhayaiś ca mṛgair yutam
viśālaiś cāgniśaraṇair yathāsthānāhitāgnibhiḥ
sakṛtprabhinnaiś ca gajair vibhūṣitaṃ
prahṛṣṭanānāvidhapakṣisevitam
supuṣpitair ambudharaprakāśair
mahīruhāṇāṃ ca vanair vicitraiḥ
nānāvṛndair vedaṣaḍaṅgapāragair
dharmābhirāmair munibhiḥ samāhitaiḥ
aśvatthaphalabhakṣāś ca tathā hy udakaśāyinaḥ
cīracarmāmbaradharās tathā valkaladhāriṇaḥ
manuṣyatānuvṛttyā tvāṃ jñātvā tiṣṭhāma sarvagam
yat tad bhagavatā pūrvaṃ dattaṃ cakraṃ tavānagha
jalāntaracaraṃ hatvā daityaṃ ca balagarvitam
utpāditaṃ vṛṣāṅkena dīptajvalanasaṃnibham
dattaṃ bhagavatā tubhyaṃ durdarśaṃ tejasādbhutam
na śakyaṃ draṣṭum anyena varjayitvā pinākinam
sudarśanaṃ bhavety evaṃ bhavenoktaṃ tadā tu tat
sudarśanaṃ tadā tasya loke nāma pratiṣṭhitam
taj jīrṇam abhavat tāta grahasyāṅgeṣu keśava
grahasyātibalasyāṅge varadattasya dhīmataḥ
na śastrāṇi vahanty aṅge cakravajraśatāny api
sadṛśaṃ bhavatety uktaṃ darśanena tathāstu tat
tatheti bhagavān āha tasya tad vacanaṃ vibhuḥ
yājñavalkya iti khyāta ṛṣiḥ paramadhārmikaḥ
ārādhya sa mahādevaṃ prāptavāñ jñānam uttamam
vedavyāsaś ca yogātmā parāśarasuto muniḥ
so 'pi śaṃkaram ārādhya prāptavān atulaṃ yaśaḥ
atrer bhāryā sutaṃ dattaṃ somaṃ durvāsasaṃ prabhoḥ
tataḥ pānīyam abhavat prasanne tryambake bhuvi
vinā bhartrā carudreṇa bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ
vikarṇaś ca mahādevaṃ tathā bhaktasukhāvaham
prasādya bhagavān siddhiṃ prāptavān madhusūdana
bhaviṣyati dvijaśreṣṭha sūtrakartā sutas tava
śakreṇa tu purā devo vārāṇasyāṃ janārdana
ārādhito 'bhūd bhaktena digvāsā bhasmaguṇṭhanaḥ
ārādhya sa mahādevaṃ devarājyam avāptavān
nāradena tu bhaktyāsau bhava ārādhitaḥ purā
tasya prīto mahādevo jagau devagurur guruḥ
tejasā tapasā kīrtyā tvatsamo na bhaviṣyati
gītenaiva ca divyena nityaṃ mām anuyāsyasi
śākalyo granthakartā ca sāvarṇiś ca tathābhavat
bāṇaḥ skandasamatvaṃ ca kāmo darpavimokṣaṇam
lavaṇo 'vadhyatām anyair daśāsyaś ca punar balam
antako 'ntam anuprāptas tasmāt ko 'nyo 'paraḥ prabhuḥ
mahādevo mayā prabhuḥ
yatnena mahatā cāpi
tadāprabhṛti caivāham arudaṃ madhusūdana
dīyatāṃ dīyatāṃ kṣīraṃ mama mātar itīritam
pitrāhaṃ yajñakāle hi nīto jñātikulaṃ mahat
tatra sā kṣarate devī divyā gauḥ suranandinī
tasyāhaṃ tat payaḥ pītvā rasena hy amṛtopamam
jñātvā kṣīraguṇāṃś caiva upalabhya hi saṃbhavam
nedaṃ kṣīraudanaṃ mātar yat tvaṃ me dattavaty asi
abhāvāc caiva dugdhasya duḥkhitā jananī tadā
āsthitānāṃ nadīṃ divyāṃ vālakhilyair niṣevitām
kutaḥ kṣīraṃ vanasthānāṃ munīnāṃ girivāsinām
pāvanānāṃ vanāśānāṃ vanāśramanivāsinām
grāmyāhāranivṛttānām āraṇyaphalabhojinām
nāsti putra payo 'raṇye surabhīgotravarjite
nadīgahvaraśaileṣu tīrtheṣu vividheṣu ca
tapasā japyanityānāṃ śivo naḥ paramā gatiḥ
sarvakāraṇabhūto 'yaṃ dhātā rudras trilocanaḥ
aṣṭabāhur jaṭī nāgabhūṣaṇaś candraśekharaḥ
nāgopavītī sagaṇo nīlakaṇṭha umāpatiḥ
sudaṃṣṭraḥ susmito nāgakuṇḍalo bhasmabhāsitaḥ
sarvakāmaprado bhaktyā pūjitaḥ puṣpamūlakaiḥ
bhaktyā pratyakṣatāṃ nītaḥ sarvakāmaprado haraḥ
jananyās tad vacaḥ śrutvā tadāprabhṛti śatruhan
prāñjaliḥ praṇato bhūtvā idam ambām acodayam
ko 'yam amba mahādevaḥ sa kathaṃ ca prasīdati
kutra vā vasate devo draṣṭavyo vā kathaṃ ca saḥ
tuṣyate vā kathaṃ śarvo rūpaṃ vā tasya kīdṛśam
kathaṃ jñeyaḥ prasanno vā darśayej jananī mama
evam uktā tadā kṛṣṇa mātā me sutavatsalā
mūrdhany āghrāya govinda sabāṣpākulalocanā
pramārjantī ca gātrāṇi mama vai madhusūdana
ambā
dhairyam ālambya jananī idam āha surottama
durvijñeyo mahādevo durārādhyo durantakaḥ
durādhṛṣyaś ca durgrāhyo durdṛśyo hy akṛtātmabhiḥ
yasya rūpāṇy anekāni pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ
sthānāni ca vicitrāṇi prasādāś cāpy anekaśaḥ
ko hi tattvena tad veda īśasya caritaṃ śubham
kṛtavān yāni rūpāṇi devadevaḥ purā kila
krīḍate ca yathā śarvaḥ prasīdati yathā ca vai
hṛdisthaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ viśvarūpo maheśvaraḥ
bhaktānām anukampārthaṃ darśanaṃ ca yathāśrutam
munīnāṃ bruvatāṃ divyām īśānacaritāṃ kathām
kṛtavān yāni rūpāṇi kathitāni divaukasaiḥ
anugrahārthaṃ viprāṇāṃ śṛṇu vatsa samāsataḥ
tāni te kīrtayiṣyāmi yan māṃ tvaṃ paripṛcchasi
brahmaviṣṇusurendrāṇāṃ rudrādityāśvinām api
viśveṣām api devānāṃ vapur dhārayate bhavaḥ
narāṇāṃ devanārīṇāṃ tathā pretapiśācayoḥ
kirātaśabarāṇāṃ ca jalajānām anekaśaḥ
karoti bhagavān rūpam āṭavyaśabarāṇy api
kūrmo matsyas tathā śaṅkhaḥ pravālāṅkurabhūṣaṇaḥ
yakṣarākṣasasarpāṇāṃ daityadānavayor api
vapur dhārayate devo bhūyaś ca bilavāsinām
vyāghrasiṃhamṛgāṇāṃ ca tarakṣvṛkṣapatatriṇām
ulūkaśvaśṛgālānāṃ rūpāṇi kurute 'pi ca
haṃsakākamayūrāṇāṃ kṛkalāsakasārasām
rūpāṇi ca balākānāṃ gṛdhracakrāṅgayor api
karoti vā sa rūpāṇi dhārayaty api parvatam
gorūpī ca mahādevo hastyaśvoṣṭrakharākṛtiḥ
chāgaśārdūlarūpaś ca anekamṛgarūpadhṛk
aṇḍajānāṃ ca divyānāṃ vapur dhārayate bhavaḥ
daṇḍī chatrī ca kuṇḍī ca dvijānāṃ dhāraṇas tathā
ṣaṇmukho vai bahumukhas trinetro bahuśīrṣakaḥ
anekakaṭipādaś ca anekodaravaktradhṛk
anekapāṇipārśvaś ca anekagaṇasaṃvṛtaḥ
ṛṣigandharvarūpaś ca siddhacāraṇarūpadhṛk
bhasmapāṇḍuragātraś ca candrārdhakṛtabhūṣaṇaḥ
anekarāvasaṃghuṣṭaś cānekastutisaṃstutaḥ
sarvabhūtāntakaḥ sarvaḥ sarvalokapratiṣṭhitaḥ
sarvalokāntarātmā ca sarvagaḥ sarvavādy api
sarvatra bhagavāñ jñeyo hṛdisthaḥ sarvadehinām
yo hi yaṃ kāmayet kāmaṃ yasminn arthe 'rcyate punaḥ
tat sarvaṃ vetti deveśas taṃ prapadya yadīcchasi
nandate kupyate cāpi tathā huṃkārayaty api
cakrī śūlī gadāpāṇir musalī khaḍgapaṭṭiśī
bhūdharo nāgamauñjī ca nāgakuṇḍalakuṇḍalī
nāgayajñopavītī ca nāgacarmottaracchadaḥ
hasate gāyate caiva nṛtyate sumanoharam
vādayaty api vādyāni vicitrāṇi gaṇair vṛtaḥ
valgate jṛmbhate caiva rudate rodayaty api
unmattamattarūpaṃ ca bhāṣate cāpi susvaraḥ
atīva hasate raudras trāsayan nayanair janam
jāgarti caiva svapiti jṛmbhate ca yathāsukham
japate japyate caiva tapate tapyate punaḥ
dadāti pratigṛhṇāti yuñjate dhyāyate 'pi ca
vedīmadhye tathā yūpe goṣṭhamadhye hutāśane
dṛśyate dṛśyate cāpi bālo vṛddho yuvā tathā
krīḍate ṛṣikanyābhir ṛṣipatnībhir eva ca
ūrdhvakeśo mahāśepho nagno vikṛtalocanaḥ
gauraḥ śyāmas tathā kṛṣṇaḥ pāṇḍuro dhūmalohitaḥ
vikṛtākṣo viśālākṣo digvāsāḥ sarvavāsakaḥ
arūpasyādyarūpasya atirūpādyarūpiṇaḥ
anādyantam ajasyāntaṃ vetsyate ko 'sya tattvataḥ
hṛdi prāṇo mano jīvo yogātmā yogasaṃjñitaḥ
dhyānaṃ tat paramātmā ca bhāvagrāhyo maheśvaraḥ
vādako gāyanaś caiva sahasraśatalocanaḥ
ekavaktro dvivaktraś ca trivaktro 'nekavaktrakaḥ
tadbhaktas tadgato nityaṃ tanniṣṭhas tatparāyaṇaḥ
bhaja putra mahādevaṃ tataḥ prāpsyasi cepsitam
evaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu divyam ārādhito mayā
ekabhakta iti jñātvā jijñāsāṃ kurute tadā
ahaṃ tam abruvaṃ tāta devendraṃ madhusūdana
satyaṃ satyaṃ hi naḥ śakra vākyam etat suniścitam
na yan maheśvaraṃ muktvā kathānyā mama rocate
satyaṃ kauśika saṃsārān mā mamāstu vinirvṛtiḥ
adya maheśvarabaddhā kathā mamānyā priyā śrotum
janma śvapākamadhye 'pi me 'stu haracaraṇavandanaratasya
mā vānīśvarabhakto bhavāmi bhavane 'pi śakrasya
vāyvambubhujo 'pi sato narasya duḥkhakṣayaḥ kutas tasya
bhavati hi surāsuragurau yasya na viśveśvare bhaktiḥ
alam anyābhis teṣāṃ kathābhir atyarthadharmayuktābhiḥ
yeṣāṃ na kṣaṇam api rucito haracaraṇasmaraṇavicchedaḥ
haracaraṇaniratamatinā bhavitavyam anārjavaṃ yugaṃ prāpya
saṃsārabhayaṃ na bhavati harabhaktirasāyanaṃ pītvā
divasaṃ divasārdhaṃ vā muhūrtaṃ vā kṣaṇaṃ lavam
na hy alabdhaprasādasya bhaktir bhavati śaṃkare
śvāpi maheśvaravacanād bhavāmi sa hi naḥ paraḥ kāmaḥ
tridaśagaṇarājyam api khalu necchāmy amaheśvarājñaptam
na nākapṛṣṭhaṃ na ca devarājyaṃ
na brahmalokaṃ na ca niṣkalatvam
na sarvakāmān akhilān vṛṇomi
harasya dāsatvam ahaṃ vṛṇomi
yadi nāma janma bhūyo bhavati madīyaiḥ punar doṣaiḥ
tasmiṃs tasmiñ janmani bhave bhaven me 'kṣayā bhaktiḥ
dhik teṣāṃ dhik teṣāṃ punar api dhig astu dhik teṣām
yeṣāṃ na vasati hṛdaye kupathagativimokṣako rudraḥ
upamanyuḥ
sad asad vyaktam avyaktaṃ yam āhur brahmavādinaḥ
nityam ekam anekaṃ ca varaṃ tasmād vṛṇomy aham
anādimadhyaparyantaṃ jñānaiśvaryam acintitam
ātmānaṃ paramaṃ yasmād varaṃ tasmād vṛṇīmahe
aiśvaryaṃ sakalaṃ yasmād anutpāditam avyayam
abījād bījasaṃbhūtaṃ varaṃ tasmād vṛṇīmahe
tamasaḥ paramaṃ jyotis tapas tadvṛttināṃ param
yaṃ jñātvā nānuśocanti varaṃ tasmād vṛṇīmahe
bhūtabhāvanabhāvajñaṃ sarvabhūtābhibhāvanam
sarvagaṃ sarvadaṃ devaṃ pūjayāmi puraṃdara
hetuvādair vinirmuktaṃ sāṃkhyayogārthadaṃ param
yam upāsanti tattvajñā varaṃ tasmād vṛṇīmahe
maghavan maghavātmānaṃ yaṃ vadanti sureśvaram
sarvabhūtaguruṃ devaṃ varaṃ tasmād vṛṇīmahe
yaḥ pūrvam asṛjad devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ lokabhāvanam
aṇḍam ākāśam āpūrya varaṃ tasmād vṛṇīmahe
agnir āpo 'nilaḥ pṛthvī khaṃ buddhiś ca mano mahān
sraṣṭā caiṣāṃ bhaved yo 'nyo brūhi kaḥ parameśvarāt
mano matir ahaṃkāras tanmātrāṇīndriyāṇi ca
brūhi caiṣāṃ bhavec chakra ko 'nyo 'sti paramaḥ śivāt
sraṣṭāraṃ bhuvanasyeha vadantīha pitāmaham
ārādhya sa tu deveśam aśnute mahatīṃ śriyam
bhagavaty uttamaiśvaryaṃ brahmaviṣṇupurogamam
vidyate vai mahādevād brūhi kaḥ parameśvarāt
daityadānavamukhyānām ādhipatyārimardanāt
ko 'nyaḥ śaknoti deveśād ṛte saṃpādituṃ sutān
dikkālasūryatejāṃsi grahavāyvindutārakāḥ
viddhi tv ete mahādevād brūhi kaḥ parameśvarāt
athotpattivināśe vā yajñasya tripurasya vā
daityadānavamukhyānām ādhipatyārimardanaḥ
kiṃ cātra bahubhiḥ sūktair hetuvādaiḥ puraṃdara
sahasranayanaṃ dṛṣṭvā tvām eva surasattama
pūjitaṃ siddhagandharvair devaiś ca ṛṣibhis tathā
devadevaprasādena tat sarvaṃ kuśikottama
avyaktamuktakeśāya sarvagasyedam ātmakam
cetanācetanādyeṣu śakra viddhi maheśvarāt
bhuvādyeṣu mahānteṣu lokālokāntareṣu ca
dvīpasthāneṣu meroś ca vibhaveṣv antareṣu ca
bhagavan maghavan devaṃ vadante tattvadarśinaḥ
yadi devāḥ surāḥ śakra paśyanty anyāṃ bhavākṛtim
kiṃ na gacchanti śaraṇaṃ marditāś cāsuraiḥ surāḥ
abhighāteṣu devānāṃ sayakṣoragarakṣasām
parasparavināśeṣu svasthānaiśvaryado bhavaḥ
andhakasyātha śumbhasya duṃdubher mahiṣasya ca
yakṣendrabalarakṣaḥsu nivātakavaceṣu ca
varadānāvaghātāya brūhi ko 'nyo maheśvarāt
surāsuraguror vaktre kasya retaḥ purā hutam
kasya vānyasya retas tad yena haimo giriḥ kṛtaḥ
digvāsāḥ kīrtyate ko 'nyo loke kaś cordhvaretasaḥ
kasya cārdhe sthitā kāntā anaṅgaḥ kena nirjitaḥ
brūhīndra paramaṃ sthānaṃ kasya devaiḥ praśasyate
śmaśāne kasya krīḍārthaṃ nṛtye vā ko 'bhibhāṣyate
kasyaiśvaryaṃ samānaṃ vā bhūtaiḥ ko vāpi krīḍate
kasya tulyabalā devagaṇā aiśvaryadarpitāḥ
ghuṣyate hy acalaṃ sthānaṃ kasya trailokyapūjitam
varṣate tapate ko 'nyo jvalate tejasā ca kaḥ
kasmād oṣadhisaṃpattiḥ ko vā dhārayate vasu
prakāmaṃ krīḍate ko vā trailokye sacarācare
jñānasiddhikriyāyogaiḥ sevyamānaś ca yogibhiḥ
ṛṣigandharvasiddhaiś ca vihitaṃ kāraṇaṃ param
karmayajñakriyāyogaiḥ sevyamānaḥ surāsuraiḥ
nityaṃ karmaphalair hīnaṃ tam ahaṃ kāraṇaṃ vade
sthūlaṃ sūkṣmam anaupamyam agrāhyaṃ guṇagocaram
guṇahīnaṃ guṇādhyakṣaṃ paraṃ māheśvaraṃ padam
viśveśaṃ kāraṇaguruṃ lokālokāntakāraṇam
bhūtābhūtabhaviṣyac ca janakaṃ sarvakāraṇam
akṣarākṣaram avyaktaṃ vidyāvidye kṛtākṛte
dharmādharmau yataḥ śakra tam ahaṃ kāraṇaṃ vade
pratyakṣam iha devendra paśya liṅgaṃ bhagāṅkitam
devadevena rudreṇa sṛṣṭisaṃhārahetunā
mātrā pūrvaṃ mamākhyātaṃ kāraṇaṃ lokakāraṇam
nāsti ceśāt paraṃ śakra taṃ prapadya yadīcchasi
pratyakṣaṃ nanu te sureśa viditaṃ saṃyogaliṅgodbhavaṃ
trailokyaṃ savikāranirguṇagaṇaṃ brahmādiretodbhavam
yad brahmendramahendraviṣṇusahitā devāś ca daityāsurā
nānyaṃ kāmasahasrakalpitadhiyaḥ śaṃsanti īśāt param
taṃ devaṃ sacarācarasya jagato vyākhyātavedyottamaṃ
kāmārthī varayāmi saṃyatamanā mokṣāya śarvaṃ śivam
na padmāṅkā na cakrāṅkā na vajrāṅkā yataḥ prajāḥ
liṅgāṅkā ca bhagāṅkā ca tasmān māheśvarī prajā
devyāḥ kāraṇarūpabhāvajanitāḥ sarvā bhagāṅkāḥ striyo
liṅgenāpi harasya sarvapuruṣāḥ pratyakṣacihnīkṛtāḥ
yo 'nyat kāraṇam īśvarāt pravadate devyā ca yan nāṅkitaṃ
trailokye sacarācare sa tu pumān bāhyo bhaved durmatiḥ
puṃliṅgaṃ sarvam īśānaṃ strīliṅgaṃ viddhi cāpy umām
dvābhyāṃ tanubhyāṃ vyāptaṃ hi carācaram idaṃ jagat
vajrasāramayaiḥ śṛṅgair niṣṭaptakanakaprabhaiḥ
tīkṣṇair amṛduraktāgrair utkirantam ivāvanim
kirīṭaṃ ca jaṭābhāraḥ sarpādyābharaṇāni ca
vajrādiśūlamātaṅgagambhīrasmitam āgatam
praśāntamanasaṃ devaṃ trinetram aparājitam
sarvavidyādhipaṃ devaṃ śaraccandrasamaprabham
nayanāhlādasaumyo 'ham apaśyaṃ parameśvaram
sahasrabhujajihvāsyo bhīṣaṇo nāgavigrahaḥ
śaṅkhaśūlāsibhiś caiva paṭṭasai rūpavān sthitaḥ
yena ca tripuraṃ dagdhaṃ sarvadevamayaḥ śaraḥ
śūlaṃ ca yauvanopetaṃ lavaṇasya kare sthitam
rāmaniḥkṣatriyakaraḥ paraśuḥ śaṃkarājñayā
śūlaṃ ca yauvanāśvaghnaṃ lavaṇasya kare sthitam
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā mahādevaṃ jānubhyām avanīṃ gataḥ
paramaṃ tīkṣṇasāreṇa śastraṃ tasyāpi yat purā
sarvabhūtagaṇāś caiva mātaro vividhāḥ sthitāḥ
jaganmūrtiṃ mahāliṅgaṃ tanmadhye sphītarūpiṇam
ayutāni ca candrārkān apaśyaṃ divi keśava
namo 'stu raktavarṇāya raktāmbaradharāya ca
raktadhvajapatākāya raktasraganulepine
namo 'stu pītavarṇāya pītāmbaradharāya ca
pītadhvajapatākāya pītasraganulepine
namo 'stūcchritachatrāya kirīṭavaradhāriṇe
ardhahārārdhakeyūraardhakuṇḍalakarṇine
namaḥ pavanavegāya namo devāya vai namaḥ
surendrāya munīndrāya mahendrāya namo 'stu te
namaḥ padmārdhamālāya utpalair miśritāya ca
ardhacandanaliptāya ardhasraganulepine
nama ādityavaktrāya ādityanayanāya ca
nama ādityavarṇāya ādityapratimāya ca
namaḥ somāya saumyāya saumyavaktradharāya ca
saumyarūpāya mukhyāya saumyadaṃṣṭrāvibhūṣaṇe
namaḥ śyāmāya gaurāya ardhapītārdhapāṇḍure
nārīnaraśarīrāya strīpuṃsāya namo 'stu te
namo 'stu vṛṣavāhāya gajendragamanāya ca
durgamāya namas tubhyam agamyāgamanāya ca
namo 'stu gaṇagītāya gaṇavṛndaratāya ca
gaṇānuyātamārgāya gaṇanityavratāya ca
namaḥ śvetābhravarṇāya saṃdhyārāgaprabhāya ca
anuddiṣṭābhidhānāya svarūpāya namo 'stu te
namo raktāgravāsāya raktasūtradharāya ca
raktamālāvicitrāya raktāmbaradharāya ca
maṇibhūṣitamūrdhāya namaś candrārdhabhūṣiṇe
vicitramaṇimūrdhāya kusumāṣṭadharāya ca
namo 'gnimukhanetrāya sahasraśaśilocane
agnirūpāya kāntāya namo 'stu gahanāya ca
khacarāya namas tubhyaṃ gocarābhiratāya ca
bhūcarāya bhuvanāya anantāya śivāya ca
namo digvāsase nityam adhivāsasuvāsase
namo jagannivāsāya pratipattisukhāya ca
nityam udbaddhamukuṭe mahākeyūradhāriṇe
sarpakaṇṭhopahārāya vicitrābharaṇāya ca
namas trinetranetrāya sahasraśatalocane
strīpuṃsāya napuṃsāya namaḥ sāṃkhyāya yogine
śaṃyor abhisravantāya atharvāya namo namaḥ
namaḥ sarvārtināśāya namaḥ śokaharāya ca
namo meghaninādāya bahumāyādharāya ca
bījakṣetrādhipālāya sraṣṭārāya namo namaḥ
namaḥ surāsureśāya viśveśāya namo namaḥ
namaḥ pavanavegāya namaḥ pavanarūpiṇe
namaḥ kāñcanamālāya girimālāya vai namaḥ
namaḥ surārimālāya caṇḍavegāya vai namaḥ
brahmaśiropahartāya mahiṣaghnāya vai namaḥ
namaḥ strīrūpadhārāya sarvarūpadharāya ca
namas tripurahartāya yajñavidhvaṃsanāya ca
namaḥ kāmāṅganāśāya kāladaṇḍadharāya ca
namaḥ skandaviśākhāya brahmadaṇḍāya vai namaḥ
namo bhavāya śarvāya viśvarūpāya vai namaḥ
īśānāya bhagaghnāya namo 'stv andhakaghātine
namo viśvāya māyāya cintyācintyāya vai namaḥ
.... .... saphalaṃ janma cādya me
surāsuragurur devo yat tiṣṭhati mamāgrataḥ
yugānte sarvabhūtāni grasann iva vyavasthitaḥ
abravaṃ tam ahaṃ bhūyaḥ praṇamya śirasā bhavam
tatas tv aham anujñāta uktavān asmi śaṃkaram
taṃ tvāṃ praṇamya śirasā prasādya prārthaye prabho
yaśasvī tejasā yukto divyajñānasamanvitaḥ
ṛṣīṇām abhigamyaś ca matprasādād bhaviṣyasi
tatra tatraiva sāṃnidhyaṃ kariṣyati na saṃśayaḥ
akṣayā bāndhavāś caiva kulaṃ gotraṃ ca te sadā
bhaviṣyati dvijaśreṣṭha mayi bhaktiś ca śāśvatī
smṛtas tvayā punar vipra kariṣyāmi ca darśanam
paśya vṛkṣalatāgulmān sarvapuṣpaphalapradān
evam etan mahādevāl labdhavān asmi keśava
prasādād devadevasya īśvarasya mahātmanaḥ
vāsudeva uvāca
etac chrutvā vacas tasya pratyakṣam iva darśanam
vismayaṃ paramaṃ gatvā abruvaṃ taṃ mahāmunim
dhanyas tvam asi viprendra kas tvad anyo 'sti puṇyakṛt
yasya devātidevas te sāṃnidhyaṃ kurute ''śrame
api tāvan mamāpy evaṃ dadyāt sa bhagavāñ śivaḥ
darśanaṃ muniśārdūla prasādaṃ cāpi śaṃkaraḥ
drakṣyase puṇḍarīkākṣa mahādevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ
acireṇaiva kālena yathā dṛṣṭo mayānagha
cakṣuṣā caiva divyena paśyāmy amitavikrama
ṣaṣṭhe māsi mahādevaṃ drakṣyase puruṣottama
ṣoḍaśāṣṭau varāṃś cāpi prāpsyasi tvaṃ maheśvarāt
sapatnīkād yaduśreṣṭha satyam etad bravīmi te
atītānāgataṃ caiva vartamānaṃ ca nityaśaḥ
viditaṃ me mahābāho prasādāt tasya dhīmataḥ
evaṃ kathayatas tasya mahādevāśritāṃ kathām
dināny aṣṭau tato jagmur muhūrtam iva bhārata
maheśvaraṃ nandivināyakādyaiḥ
ekādaśā gatāś caiva rudrāś cainaṃ sukhāvaham
vṛkṣauṣadhyaś ca rājendra dhātavo vividhās tathā
tvayā hy ārādhitaś cāhaṃ śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ
tvatsamo nāsti me kaś cit triṣu lokeṣu vai priyaḥ
tato 'stuvaṃ devadevaṃ devīṃ ca śubhalakṣaṇām
yogeśvarāḥ subahavo maheśaṃ bahudhāstuvan
mahābhūtāni cchandāṃsi prajānāṃ patayo makhāḥ
saritaḥ sāgarā nāgā gandharvāpsarasas tathā
vidyādharāś ca gītena vādyanṛttādinārcayan
tejasvināṃ madhyagataṃ tejorāśiṃ jagatpatim
īkṣituṃ ca mahādevaṃ na me śaktir abhūt tadā
tato devena paśyeti vadasvety abhibhāṣitam
dṛṣṭvā devaṃ tathā devīm astuvaṃ saṃstutaṃ suraiḥ
tvayā sṛṣṭam idaṃ kṛtsnaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram
tattvavid brāhmaṇo vidvān aprameyagatis tathā
mūrdhnā praṇamya tu bhavaṃ devam īśānam avyayam
bhavantu nityam atithīnāṃ bahūni
gṛhe ca tubhyaṃ prītimatāṃ śatāni
iti taṇḍis tapoyogāt paramātmānam avyayam
cintayitvā mahātmānam idam āha suvismitaḥ
yaṃ paṭhanti sadā sāṃkhyāś cintayanti ca yoginaḥ
paraṃ pradhānaṃ puruṣam adhiṣṭhātāram īśvaram
utpattau ca vināśe ca kāraṇaṃ yaṃ vidur budhāḥ
devāsuramunīnāṃ ca paraṃ yasmān na vidyate
ajaṃ tam aham īśānam anādinidhanaṃ prabhum
atyantasukhinaṃ devam anaghaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje
evaṃ bruvann eva tadā dadarśa tapasāṃ nidhim
tam avyayam anaupamyam acintyaṃ śāśvataṃ dhruvam
niṣkalaṃ sakalaṃ brahma nirguṇaṃ guṇagocaram
yogināṃ paramānandam akṣaraṃ mokṣasaṃjñitam
manorindrāgnimarutāṃ viśvasya brahmaṇo gatim
agrāhyam acalaṃ śuddhaṃ buddhigrāhyaṃ manomayam
durvijñeyam asaṃkhyeyaṃ duṣprāpam akṛtātmabhiḥ
yoniṃ viśvasya jagatas tamasaḥ parataḥ param
paraṃ purāṇaṃ puruṣaṃ parebhyaḥ parataḥ param
yaḥ prāṇavantam ātmānaṃ jyotir jīvasthitaṃ manaḥ
taṃ devaṃ darśanākāṅkṣī bahūn varṣagaṇān ṛṣiḥ
tapasy ugre sthito bhūtvā dṛṣṭvā tuṣṭāva ceśvaram
oṃkāraratham āruhya te viśanti maheśvaram
ṛtaṃ satyaṃ paraṃ brahma stuvanty ātharvaṇā dvijāḥ
upamanyur uvāca
evaṃ stuto mahādevas taṇḍinā brahmavādinā
uvāca bhagavān deva umayā sahitaḥ prabhuḥ
mahāroṣo mahārūpo makhas tribhuvaneśvaraḥ
satyaḥ satyāśrayaḥ siddhaś caṇḍāṃśuḥ sarpakuṇḍalaḥ
vṛṣaś ca vṛṣarūpaś ca vṛṣastho vṛṣabhākṣagaḥ
bhīmaś ca śitikaṇṭhaś ca śivaḥ śvetaḥ śivottamaḥ
niṣaṅgī śitikeśaś ca ditipo nīlalohitaḥ
śarabhaḥ śaṅkukarṇaś ca vilāso viśvakartṛkaḥ
paraḥ pāśupato bhadro nīlagrīvo vibhīṣaṇaḥ
gaṅgādharaḥ pṛthuś caiva janeśvarakuleśvaraḥ
uttamastrīsahāyaś ca madhuvindo mahāgrahaḥ
divyaḥ sūkṣmaḥ parāgaś ca vīrabhadro vibhinnagaḥ
śivaḥ śaṃbhur bhuvo bhartā śrīkaṇṭhas tridaśārcitaḥ
kravyādaḥ kīrtimān krīḍī kālāgniḥ kāryakāraṇaḥ
aṇḍaś ca bahiraṇḍaś ca virāḍbhūto viśeṣakṛt
hiraṇyagarbhahetuś ca bahirbhūtaḥ prayojakaḥ
jāgratsvapnasuṣuptiś ca turīyaś ca vidhūtanaḥ
manobuddhir ahaṃkāro vyaktāntaḥkaraṇātmakaḥ
viśado vikramaś caiva viśvo viśvasya saṃgrahaḥ
arūpaś caṇḍarūpaś ca vajrahastaḥ puraṃdaraḥ
jātavedāś ca jāgartir viśvāvāso vināyakaḥ
ājānur vainateyaś ca viśvakarmā vicakṣaṇaḥ
hiraṇyākṣaḥ sukeśaś ca ṛkṣaḥ kakṣataras tamaḥ
vṛṣo vṛṣāḍhyo vṛṣado vṛṣaparvā prajāharaḥ
sakārmuko mahābāhur mahāghoro mahātapāḥ
gambhīraghoṣo yogātmā yajñahā kāmanāśanaḥ
sutīkṣṇadaśanaś caiva mahākāyo mahānanaḥ
viṣvakseno harir yajñaḥ saṃyugāpīḍavāhanaḥ
vīṇī ca paṇavī tālī nālī kaṭikaṭus tathā
praskando 'py avibhāvaś ca tulyo yajñavibhāgavit
sarvakāmavaraś caiva sarvadā sarvatomukhaḥ
bhikṣuś ca bhikṣurūpaś ca raudrarūpo 'ṅgirāḥ khagaḥ
vasuraśmiḥ suvarcasvī vasuvego mahābalaḥ
manovego niśācāraḥ sarvalokasukhapradaḥ
samīro damanākāro hy artho hy arthakaro vaśaḥ
vāsudevaś ca devaś ca vāmadevaś ca vāmanaḥ
nimittastho nimittaṃ ca nandir nandikaro hariḥ
vajrahastaḥ pratiṣṭambhī camūruḥ stena eva ca
nityo hy anīśaḥ śuddhātmā śuddho māno gatir haviḥ
mahākaṭir mahāgrīvo mahābāhur mahākaraḥ
lambamāno lambitoṣṭhaś caladgāmī yaśonidhiḥ
māyāvī sarvakāmakṛt
mahādayāḷur mantavyo
prāṇeśo bandhakī vṛkṣo nakulaś cādrikas tathā
hrasvagrīvo mahājānur alolaś ca mahauṣadhiḥ
bhūtālayo bhūtapatir ahorātro malo 'malaḥ
vasubhṛt sarvabhūtātmā niścalaḥ suvidur budhaḥ
asuhṛt sarvabhūtānāṃ niścalaś calavid budhaḥ
śirodharo 'bhimarśanaḥ sarvalakṣaṇabhūṣitaḥ
girāvāso visargaś ca sarvalakṣaṇalakṣavit
idam amṛtam anantam aprameyaṃ
sakalajanārtiharaṃ samastavedyam
japata bahubhir īritaṃ viśeṣyaṃ
śivapadamantravighaṭṭanena tīvram
namaḥ śivāyeti sadā pradānaṃ bhagavān iti
bhaktyā stuto mayā yat tad vidus tattvaṃ surarṣabhāḥ
bhaktyā hy ananyam īśānaṃ paraṃ devaṃ sanātanam
karmaṇā manasā vācā bhāvenāmitatejasaḥ
svapanto jāgramāṇāś ca vrajann upaviśaṃs tathā
unmiṣan nimiṣaṃś caiva cintayantaḥ punaḥ punaḥ
śṛṇvantaḥ śrāvayantaś ca kathayantaś ca te bhavam
stavam etadgato brahmā svayam ārādhayan prajāḥ
gīyate ca sa budhyeta brahmā śaṃkarasaṃnidhau
idaṃ puṇyaṃ pavitraṃ ca sarvadā pāpanāśanam
yogadaṃ mokṣadaṃ caiva svargadaṃ toṣadaṃ tathā
evam etat paṭhante ya ekabhaktyā tu śaṃkare
yā gatiḥ sāṃkhyayogānāṃ vrajanty etāṃ gatiṃ tadā
stavam etaṃ prayatnena sadā rudrasya saṃnidhau
abdam ekaṃ cared bhaktaḥ prāpnuyād īpsitaṃ phalam
etad rahasyaṃ paramaṃ brahmaṇo hṛdi saṃsthitam
kapilaś ca tataḥ prāha sāṃkhyarṣir devasaṃmataḥ
mayā janmāny anekāni śaktyā cārādhito bhavaḥ
prītaś ca bhagavāñ jñānaṃ dadau mama bhavāntakam
viśvāmitras tadovāca kṣatriyo 'haṃ purābhavam
brāhmaṇo 'haṃ bhavānīti mayā cārādhito bhavaḥ
tatprasādān mayā prāptaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ durlabhaṃ mahat
vāsudevas tadovāca punar matimatāṃ varaḥ
suvarṇākṣo mahādevas tapasā toṣito mayā
tato 'tha bhagavān āha prīto māṃ vai yudhiṣṭhira
annāt priyataraḥ kṛṣṇa matprasādād bhaviṣyasi
aparājitaś ca yuddheṣu tejaś caivānalopamam
evaṃ sahasraśaś cātmā mahādevo varaṃ dadau
maṇimanthe 'tha śaile vai purā saṃrādhito mayā
varṣāyutasahasrāṇāṃ sahasraśatam eva ca
tato māṃ bhagavān prīta idaṃ vacanam abravīt
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te yas te manasi vartate
tataḥ praṇamya śirasā idaṃ vacanam abruvam
yadi prīto mahādevo bhaktyā paramayā prabhuḥ
nityakālaṃ taveśāna bhaktir bhavatu me sthirā
evam astv iti bhagavāṃs tatroktvāntaradhīyata
jaigīṣavyas tathā prāha jagaddhātā maheśvaraḥ
parāśaraś cāha muniḥ purā mama ca toṣitaḥ
tad abhijñāya mām āha śaṃkaras tripurārdanaḥ
pitṛkanyā vasoḥ putrī śāpād dāśagṛhoditā
tasyāṃ viṣṇukṛtā nāma vidyayā śodhakaḥ satām
māṇḍavyaś cāha deveśa bhakto lokahite bhave
amarṣaṃ saphalaṃ kartuṃ śakto nirvighnadharmabhāk
vāsudeva uvāca
upamanyur mayi prāha tapann iva divākaraḥ
aśubhaiḥ pāpakarmāṇo ye narāḥ kaluṣīkṛtāḥ
īśānaṃ na prapadyante tamorājasavṛttayaḥ
īśvaraṃ saṃprapadyante dvijā bhāvitabhāvanāḥ
sarvathā vartamāno 'pi yo bhaktaḥ parameśvare
sadṛśo 'raṇyavāsīnāṃ munīnāṃ bhāvitātmanām
brahmatvaṃ keśavatvaṃ vā śakratvaṃ vā suraiḥ saha
trailokyasyādhipatyaṃ vā tuṣṭo rudraḥ prayacchati
manasāpi śivaṃ tāta ye prapadyanti mānavāḥ
vidhūya sarvapāpāni devaiḥ saha vasanti te
bhittvā chittvā ca kūlāni hatvā sarvam idaṃ jagat
yajed devaṃ virūpākṣaṃ na sa pāpena lipyate
sarvalakṣaṇahīno 'pi yukto vā sarvapātakaiḥ
sarvaṃ tudati tat pāpaṃ bhāvayañ chivam ātmanā
kīṭapakṣipataṃgānāṃ tiraścām api keśava
mahādevaprapannānāṃ na bhayaṃ vidyate kva cit
evam eva mahādevaṃ bhaktā ye mānavā bhuvi
na te saṃsāravaśagā iti me niścitā matiḥ
tataḥ kṛṣṇo 'bravīd vākyaṃ dharmaputraṃ yudhiṣṭhiram
ananyastrījanaḥ prājño hy apravāsī priyaṃvadaḥ
surūpaḥ saṃmato vīraḥ śīlavān bhogabhuk śuciḥ
dārānumatayajñaś ca sunakṣatrām athodvahet
sabhṛtyaḥ svajanopeta iha pretya ca modate
kailāse śaṃkarāvāsam abhivīkṣya praṇamya ca
gaurīśaṃ śaṃkaraṃ dāntaṃ śaraṇāgatavatsalam
gaṅgādharaṃ gopatigaṃ gaṇāvṛtam akalmaṣam
tayā saṃpūjitas tatra śayane cātinirmale
śuddhakṣetre brahmahatyā prāyaścittam athocyate
punaś ca pātakaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ viprakṣetre viśeṣataḥ
prāyaścittaṃ mahad ato dāragrahaṇapūrvakam
bījaṃ na śudhyate voḍhur asya cākṛtaniṣkṛteḥ
mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaḥ śuddho jñeyaḥ putro yathārthataḥ
saṃdhyopāsanam ity āho sarvapāpaharaṃ name
bhuñjato 'bhyāgamad ahas tataḥ saṃdhyābhavat prabho
pṛthak caiva tathā suptau sā strī sa ca munis tadā
athārdharātre sā strī tu śayanaṃ tad upāgamat
tvayy āveśitacittā ca svatantrāsmi bhajasva mām
anyat karma na cānyasya svātantryaṃ tan na yujyate
na vṛddhām akṣamāṃ manye na cecchā tvayi me 'naghe
aṣṭāvakro 'tha sa muniś cintayām āsa vismitaḥ
aviśvasan na vyasanī nātisakto 'pravāsakaḥ
vidvān suśīlaḥ puruṣaḥ sadāraḥ sukham aśnute
nikṛtiḥ strī bhogaparā priyavādāpravāsanāt
rakṣyate ca kucelādyair aprasaṅgānuvartanaiḥ
aparvāsv aniṣiddhāsu rātriṣv apy anṛtau vrajet
rātrau ca nātiniyamo na vai hy aniyamo bhavet
yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
vaiśyayonyāṃ samutpannāḥ śūdrayonyāṃ tathaiva ca
brahmarṣaya iti proktāḥ purāṇā dvijasattamāḥ
katham etan mahārāja tattvaṃ śaṃsitum arhasi
bhīṣmaḥ
viruddham iha paśyāmi viyonau brāhmaṇo bhavet
alaṃ kautūhalenātra nibodha tvaṃ yudhiṣṭhira
śubhetaraṃ śubhaṃ vāpi na cintayitum arhasi
īśanty ātmana ity etad gatiś caiṣāṃ na sajjate
brahmabhūyāṃsa ity eva ṛṣayaḥ śruticoditāḥ
nindyā na caite rājendra pramāṇaṃ hi pramāṇinām
loko 'numanyate caitān pramāṇaṃ hy atra vai tapaḥ
evaṃ mahātmabhis tāta tapojñānasamanvitaiḥ
pravartitāni kāryāṇi pramāṇāny eva sattama
bhāryāś catasro rājendra brāhmaṇasya svadharmataḥ
brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śūdrā ca bharatarṣabha
rājñāṃ tu kṣatriyā vaiśyā śūdrā ca bharatarṣabha
vaiśyasya vaiśyā vihitā śūdrā ca bharatarṣabha
śūdrasyaikā smṛtā bhāryā pratilome tu saṃkaraḥ
śūdrāyās tu naraśreṣṭha catvāraḥ patayaḥ smṛtāḥ
varṇottamāyās tu patiḥ savarṇas tv eka eva saḥ
dvau kṣatriyāyā vihitau brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyas tathā
vaiśyāyās tu naraśreṣṭha vihitāḥ patayas trayaḥ
savarṇaḥ kṣatriyaś caiva brāhmaṇaś ca viśāṃ pate
evaṃ vidhim anusmṛtya tatas te ṛṣibhiḥ purā
utpāditā mahātmānaḥ putrā brahmarṣayaḥ purā
purāṇābhyām ṛṣibhyāṃ tu mitreṇa varuṇena ca
vasiṣṭho 'tha tathāgastyo barhiṣadbhis tathaiva ca
kakṣīvān ārṣṭiṣeṇaś ca puruṣaḥ kakṣa eva ca
māmateyasya vai putrā gautamasyātmajāḥ smṛtāḥ
vatsapriyaś ca bhagavān sthūlaraśmis tathākṣaṇiḥ
gautamasyaiva tanayā ye dāsyāṃ janitā hy uta
kapiṃjalādo brahmarṣiś caṇḍālyām udapadyata
vainateyas tathā pakṣī turyāyāṃ ca vasiṣṭhataḥ
prasādāc ca vasiṣṭhasya śulkābhyupagamena ca
adṛśyantyāḥ pitā vaiśyo nāmnā citramukhaḥ purā
brāhmaṇatvam anuprāpto brahmarṣitvaṃ ca kaurava
vaiśyaś citramukhaḥ kanyāṃ vasiṣṭhatanayasya vai
śubhāṃ prādād yato jāto brahmarṣis tu parāśaraḥ
tathaiva dāśakanyāyāṃ satyavatyāṃ mahān ṛṣiḥ
parāśarāt prasūtaś ca vyāso yogamayo muniḥ
vibhaṇḍakasya mṛgyāṃ ca tapoyogātmako muniḥ
ṛśyaśṛṅgaḥ samutpanno brahmacārī mahāyaśāḥ
śāṅgaryāṃ ca mandapālasya catvāro brahmavādinaḥ
jātā brahmarṣayaḥ puṇyā yaiḥ stuto havyavāhanaḥ
droṇaś ca stambamitraś ca śārisṛkvaś ca buddhimān
jaritāriś ca vikhyātāś catvāraḥ sūryasaṃnibhāḥ
maharṣeḥ kālavṛkṣasya śakuntyām eva jajñivān
hiraṇyahasto bhagavān maharṣiḥ kāñcanaprabhaḥ
pāvakāt tāta saṃbhūtā manasā ca maharṣayaḥ
pitāmahasya rājendra pulastyapulahādayaḥ
savarṇaś cāpi rājarṣiḥ savarṇāyām ajāyata
mṛṇmayyāṃ bharataśreṣṭha ādityena vivasvatā
śāṇḍilyaś cāgnito jātaḥ kāśyapasyātmajaḥ prabhuḥ
śaradvataḥ śarastambāt kṛpaś ca kṛpayā saha
padmāc ca jajñe rājendra somapasya mahātmanaḥ
reṇuś ca reṇukā caiva rāmamātā yaśasvinī
yamunāyāṃ samudbhūtaḥ somakena mahātmanā
arkadatto mahān ṛṣiḥ prathitaḥ pṛthivītale
agner āhavanīyāc ca drupadasyendravarcasaḥ
dhṛṣṭadyumnaś ca saṃbhūto vedyāṃ kṛṣṇā ca bhārata
vyāghrayonyāṃ tato jātā vasiṣṭhasya mahātmanaḥ
ekonaviṃśatiḥ putrāḥ khyātā vyāghrapadādayaḥ
mandhaś ca bādalomaś ca jābāliś ca mahān ṛṣiḥ
manyuś caivopamanyuś ca setukarṇas tathaiva ca
ete cānye ca vikhyātāḥ pṛthivyāṃ gotratāṃ gatāḥ
viśvakāśasya rājarṣer aikṣvākos tu mahātmanaḥ
bālāśvo nāma putro 'bhūc chikhāṃ bhittvā viniḥsṛtaḥ
māndhātā caiva rājarṣir yuvanāśvena dhīmatā
svayaṃ dhṛto 'tha garbheṇa divyāstrabalasaṃyutaḥ
gaurikaś cāpi rājarṣiś cakravartī mahāyaśāḥ
bāhudāyāṃ samutpanno nadyāṃ rājñā subāhunā
bhūmeś ca putro narakaḥ saṃvartaś caiva puṣkalaḥ
adbhiś caiva mahātejā ṛṣir gārgyo hy ajāyata
ete cānye ca bahavo rājanyā brāhmaṇās tathā
prabhāvenābhisaṃbhūtā maharṣīṇāṃ mahātmanām
nāsādhyaṃ tapasā teṣāṃ vidyayātmaguṇaiḥ paraiḥ
asminn arthe ca manunā gītaḥ śloko narādhipa
dharmaṃ praṇayatā rājaṃs taṃ nibodha yudhiṣṭhira
ṛṣīṇāṃ ca nadīnāṃ ca sādhūnāṃ ca mahātmanām
prabhavo nādhigantavyaḥ strīṇāṃ duścaritasya ca
tan nātra cintā kartavyā maharṣīṇāṃ samudbhave
yathā sarvagato hy agnis tathā tejo mahātmasu
yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
putraiḥ kathaṃ mahārāja puruṣas tārito bhavet
yāvan na labdhavān putram aphalaḥ puruṣo nṛpa
bhīṣmaḥ
atrāpy udāharantīmam itihāsaṃ purātanam
nāradena purā gītaṃ mārkaṇḍeyāya pṛcchate
parvataṃ nāradaṃ caiva asitaṃ devalaṃ ca tam
āraṇeyaṃ ca raibhyaṃ ca etān atrāgatān purā
gaṅgāyamunayor madhye bhogavatyāḥ samāgame
dṛṣṭvā pūrvaṃ samāsīnān mārkaṇḍeyo 'bhyagacchata
ṛṣayas tu muniṃ dṛṣṭvā samutthāyonmukhāḥ sthitāḥ
arcayitvārhato vipraṃ kiṃ kurma iti cābruvan
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
ayaṃ samāgamaḥ sadbhir yatnenāsādito mayā
atra prāpsyāmi dharmāṇām ācārasya ca niścayam
ṛjuḥ kṛtayuge dharmas tasmin kṣīṇe vimuhyati
yuge yuge maharṣibhyo dharmam icchāmi veditum
ṛṣibhir nāradaḥ prokto brūhi yatrāsya saṃśayaḥ
dharmādharmeṣu tattvajña tvaṃ hi cchettāsi saṃśayān
ṛṣibhyo 'numataṃ vākyaṃ niyogān nārado 'bravīt
sarvadharmārthatattvajñaṃ mārkaṇḍeyaṃ tato 'bravīt
dīrghāyo tapasā dīpta vedavedāṅgatattvavit
yatra te saṃśayo brahman samutpannaḥ sa ucyatām
dharmaṃ lokopakāraṃ vā yac cānyac chrotum icchasi
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
tad ahaṃ kathayiṣyāmi brūhi tvaṃ sumahātapaḥ
yuge yuge vyatīte 'smin dharmasetuḥ praṇaśyati
nāradaḥ
kathaṃ dharmacchalenāhaṃ prāpnuyām iti me matiḥ
āsīd dharmaḥ purā vipra catuṣpādaḥ kṛte yuge
tato hy adharmaḥ kālena prasūtaḥ kiṃ cid unnataḥ
tatas tretāyugaṃ nāma pravṛttaṃ dharmadūṣaṇam
tasmin vyatīte saṃprāptaṃ tṛtīyaṃ dvāparaṃ yugam
tadā dharmasya dvau pādāv adharmo nāśayiṣyati
dvāpare tu parikṣīṇe nandike samupasthite
lokavṛttaṃ ca dharmaṃ ca ucyamānaṃ nibodha me
caturthaṃ nandikaṃ nāma dharmapādāv aśeṣataḥ
tataḥprabhṛti jāyante kṣīṇaprajñāyuṣo narāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
kṣīṇaprāṇadhanā loke dharmācārabahiṣkṛtāḥ
evaṃ vilulite dharme loke cādharmasaṃyute
nāradaḥ
cāturvarṇyasya niyataṃ havyakavyaṃ niyacchati
mantrapūtaṃ sadā havyaṃ kavyaṃ caiva na naśyati
pratigṛhṇanti tad devā dātur nyāyāt prayacchataḥ
sattvayuktaṃ ca dātā ca sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
avāptakāmaḥ svarge ca mahīyeta yathepsitam
catvāro hy atha ye varṇā havyakavyaṃ pradāsyate
nāradaḥ
mantrahīnam apanyāyaṃ teṣāṃ dattaṃ kva gacchati
asurān gacchate dattaṃ viprai rakṣāṃsi kṣatriyaiḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
vaiśyaiḥ pretāni vai dattaṃ śūdrair bhūtāni gacchati
atha varṇāvare jātāś cāturvarṇyāpadeśinaḥ
nāradaḥ
dāsyanti havyakavyāni teṣāṃ dattaṃ kva gacchati
varṇāvarāṇāṃ bhūtānāṃ havyakavyapradātṛṇām
naiva devā na pitaraḥ pratigṛhṇanti tat svayam
yātudhānāḥ piśācāś ca bhūtā ye cāpi nairṛtāḥ
teṣāṃ sā vihitā vṛttiḥ pitṛdaivatanirgatā
teṣāṃ sarvapradātṝṇāṃ havyakavyaṃ samāhitāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
yat prayacchanti vidhivat tad vai bhuñjanti devatāḥ
śrutaṃ varṇāvare dattaṃ havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca nārada
nāradaḥ
saṃprayoge ca putrāṇāṃ kanyānāṃ ca bravīhi me
kanyāpradānaṃ putrāṇāṃ strīṇāṃ saṃyogam eva ca
ānupūrvyān mayā samyag ucyamānaṃ nibodha me
jātamātrā tu dātavyā kanyakā sadṛśe vare
kāladattāsu kanyāsu pitā dharmeṇa yujyate
yas tu puṣpavatīṃ kanyāṃ bāndhavo na prayacchati
māsi māsi gate bandhus tasyā bhrauṇaghnyam āpnute
yas tu kanyāṃ gṛhe rundhyād grāmyair bhogair vivarjitām
avadhyātaḥ sa kanyāyā bandhuḥ prāpnoti bhrūṇahām
dūṣitā pāṇimātreṇa mṛte bhartari dārikā
saṃskāraṃ labhate nārī dvitīye sā punaḥ patau
punar bhūr nāma sā kanyā saputrā havyakavyadā
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
adūṣyā sā prasūtīṣu prajānāṃ dārakarmaṇi
yā tu kanyā prasūyeta garbhiṇī yā tu vā bhavet
atha dārakriyāṃ bhūyaḥ sā bhajed ṛṣisattama
nāradaḥ
tattvārthaniścitaṃ śabdaṃ kanyakā nayate 'gnaye
tasmāt kurvanti vai bhāvaṃ kumāryas tā na kanyakāḥ
brahmahatyātribhāgena garbhādhānaviśodhitām
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
gṛhṇīyāt tāṃ caturbhāgaṃ viśuddhāṃ sarjanāt punaḥ
kathaṃ kanyāsu ye jātā bandhūnāṃ dūṣitāḥ sadā
kasya te havyakavyāni pradāsyanti mahāmune
nāradaḥ
kanyāyās tu pituḥ putrāḥ kānīnā havyakavyadāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
antarvatnyās tu yaḥ pāṇiṃ gṛhṇīyāt sa sahoḍhajaḥ
atha yenāhito garbhaḥ kanyāyāṃ tatra nārada
nāradaḥ
kathaṃ putraphalaṃ tasya bhaved etat pracakṣva me
dharmācāreṣu te nityaṃ dūṣakāḥ kṛtaśodhanāḥ
bījaṃ ca naśyate teṣāṃ moghaceṣṭā bhavanti te
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
atha kā cid bhavet kanyā krītā dattā hṛtāpi vā
nāradaḥ
kathaṃ putrakṛtaṃ tasyās tad bhaved ṛṣisattama
krītā dattā hṛtā caiva yā kanyā pāṇivarjitā
kaumārī nāma sā bhāryā prasaved aurasān sutān
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
na patyarthe śubhā proktā tatkarmaṇy aparājite
kena maṅgalakṛtyeṣu viniyujyanti kanyakāḥ
nāradaḥ
etad icchāmi vijñātuṃ tattveneha mahāmune
nityaṃ nivasate lakṣmīḥ kanyakāsu pratiṣṭhitā
śobhanā śubhayogyā ca pūjyā maṅgalakarmasu
ākarasthaṃ yathā ratnaṃ sarvakāmaphalopagam
tathā kanyā mahālakṣmīḥ sarvalokasya maṅgalam
evaṃ kanyā parā lakṣmī ratis toṣaś ca dehinām
mahākulānāṃ cāritraṃ vṛttena nikaṣopalam
ānayitvā svakād varṇāt kanyakāṃ yo bhajen naraḥ
dātāraṃ havyakavyānāṃ putrakaṃ sā prasūyate
sādhvī kulaṃ vardhayati sādhvī puṣṭir gṛhe parā
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
sādhvī lakṣmī ratiḥ sākṣāt pratiṣṭhā saṃtatis tathā
śrutaṃ bahuvidhaṃ vṛttaṃ kanyakānāṃ mahāmate
icchāmi yoṣitāṃ śrotuṃ dharmādharmaṃ parigrahe
nāradaḥ
aṣṭau bhāryāgamā dharmyā narāṇāṃ dārakarmaṇi
pretyeha ca hitā yās tu saputrā havyakavyadāḥ
sādhvī pāṇigṛhītā yā kaumārī pāṇivarjitā
bhrātṛbhāryā svabhāryeti prasūyet putram aurasam
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
trayo bhāryāgamā jñātā yatra dharmo na naśyati
nāradaḥ
pañcānyāḥ paścimā brūhi bhāryās tāsāṃ ca ye sutāḥ
sagotrabhāryā krītā ca parabhāryā ca kāritā
gatāgatā ca yā bhāryā āśramād āhṛtā ca yā
ete bhāryāgamāḥ pañca punar bhāryā bhavanti yāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
etā bhāryā nṛṇāṃ gamyās tatputrā havyakavyadāḥ
śrutā bhāryāś ca putrāś ca vistareṇa mahāmune
nāradaḥ
āśramasthāḥ kathaṃ nāryo na duṣyantīti brūhi me
āśramasthāsu nārīṣu bāndhavatvaṃ praṇaśyati
naṣṭavaṃśyā bhavanty etā bandhūnām atha bhartṛṇām
paradārā muktadoṣās tā nāryo ''śramasaṃsthitāḥ
svayam īśāḥ svadehānāṃ kāmyās tadgatamānasāḥ
evaṃ nāryo na duṣyanti narāṇāṃ tatprasūtiṣu
dharmapatnyo bhavanty etāḥ saputrā havyakavyadāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
parasya bhāryā yā pūrvaṃ mṛte bhartari yā punaḥ
nāradaḥ
anyaṃ bhajati bhartāraṃ sasutā asutā katham
asūtā vā prasūtā vā gṛhasthānāṃ parastriyaḥ
parāmṛṣṭeti tā varjyā dharmācāreṣu dūṣitāḥ
na cāsāṃ havyakavyāni pratigṛhṇanti devatāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
yas tāsu janayet putrān na taiḥ putram avāpnuyāt
parakṣetreṣu yo bījaṃ cāpalyād visṛjen naraḥ
nāradaḥ
kathaṃ putraphalaṃ tasya bhavet tad ṛṣisattama
asvāmike parakṣetre yo naro bījam utsṛjet
svayaṃvṛtto ''śramasthāyāṃ tad bījaṃ na vinaśyati
parakṣetreṣu yo bījaṃ naro darpāt samutsṛjet
kṣetrikasyaiva tad bījaṃ na bījī labhate phalam
nātaḥ param adharmyaṃ cāpy ayaśasyaṃ tathottaram
garbhādīnāṃ ca bahubhis tāś ca tyājyāḥ sameṣv api
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
atha ye paradāreṣu putrā jāyanti nārada
nāradaḥ
kasya te bandhudāyādā bhavanti paramadyute
paradāreṣu jāyete dvau putrau kuṇḍagolakau
jīvaty atha patau kuṇḍo mṛte bhartari golakaḥ
te ca jātāḥ parakṣetre dehināṃ pretya ceha ca
dattāni havyakavyāni nāśayanty atha dātṛṇām
pitur hi narakāyaite golakas tu viśeṣataḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
caṇḍālatulyau tajjau hi paratreha ca naśyataḥ
kasya te garhitāḥ putrāḥ pitṝṇāṃ havyakavyadāḥ
nāradaḥ
yasya kṣetre prasūyante yo vā tāñ janayet sutān
kṣetrikaś caiva bījī ca dvāv etau nirayaṃ gatau
na rakṣati hi yo dārān paradārāṃś ca gacchati
garhitās te narā nityaṃ dharmācārabahiṣkṛtāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
kuṇḍo bhoktā ca bhojī ca kutsitāḥ pitṛdaivataiḥ
tathaite garhitāḥ putrā havyakavyāni nārada
nāradaḥ
kasya nityaṃ prayacchanti dharmo vā teṣu kiṃ phalam
yātudhānāḥ piśācāś ca pratigṛhṇanti tair hutam
havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca tair dattaṃ ye ca bhūtā niśācarāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
atha te rākṣasāḥ prītāḥ kiṃ prayacchanti dātṛṇām
nāradaḥ
kiṃ vā dharmaphalaṃ teṣāṃ bhavet tad ṛṣisattama
na dattaṃ naśyate kiṃ cit sarvabhūteṣu dātṛṇām
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
pretya ceha ca tāṃ puṣṭim upāśnanti pradāyinaḥ
atha golakakuṇḍābhyāṃ saṃtatir yā bhaviṣyati
tayor ye bāndhavāḥ ke cit pradāsyanti kathaṃ nu tat
nāradaḥ
sādhvījātāḥ sutās teṣāṃ tāṃ vṛttim anutiṣṭhatām
prīṇanti pitṛdaivatyaṃ havyakavyasamāhitāḥ
evaṃ golakakuṇḍābhyāṃ ye ca varṇāpadeśinaḥ
havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca śuddhānāṃ pratigṛhṇanti devatāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
śrutaṃ narāṇāṃ cāpalyaṃ parastrīṣu prajāyatām
nāradaḥ
pramadānāṃ tu cāpalye doṣam icchāmi veditum
ekavarṇe 'pi doṣaṃ tu gamanaṃ pūrvakālikam
dhātā ca samanujñāto viṣṇunā tat tathākarot
bhagaliṅge mahāprājña pūrvam eva prajāpatiḥ
sasarja tābhyāṃ saṃyogam anujñātaś cakāra saḥ
atha viṣṇuprasādena bhago dattavaraḥ kila
tena caiva prasādena sarvāṃl lokān upāśnute
tasmāt tu puruṣe doṣo hy adhiko nātra saṃśayaḥ
vinā garbhaṃ savarṇeṣu na tyājyā gamanāt striyaḥ
prāyaścittaṃ yathānyāyaṃ daṇḍaṃ kuryāt sa paṇḍitaḥ
śvabhir vā daśanaṃ snānaṃ savanatritayaṃ niśi
bhūmau ca bhasmaśayanaṃ dānaṃ bhogavivarjitam
doṣagauravataḥ kālo dravyagauravam eva ca
maryādā sthāpitā pūrvam iti tīrthāntaraṃ gate
tad yoṣitāṃ tu dīrghāyo nāsti doṣo vyatikrame
bhagastīrthāntare śuddho viṣṇos tu vacanād iha
rakṣyāś caivānyasaṃvādair anyagehād vimokṣaṇaiḥ
āsāṃ śuddhau viśeṣeṇa karmaṇāṃ phalam aśnute
naitā vācyā na vai vadhyā na kleśyāḥ śubham icchatā
viṣṇuprasādād ity eva bhagastīrthāntaraṃ gataḥ
māsi māsi rajas tāsāṃ duṣkṛtāny apakarṣati
striyas toṣakarā nṝṇāṃ striyas tuṣṭipradāḥ sadā
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
putrasetupratiṣṭhāś ca striyo loke mahādyute
śrutaṃ balaṃ prabhāvaś ca yoṣitāṃ munisattama
nāradaḥ
ekasya bahubhāryasya dharmam icchāmi veditum
bahubhāryāsu saktasya nārībhogeṣu dehinaḥ
ṛtau vimucyamānasya sāṃnidhye bhrūṇahā smṛtaḥ
vṛddhāṃ vandhyāṃ suvratāṃ ca mṛtāpatyām apuṣpiṇīm
kanyāṃ ca bahuputrāṃ ca varjayan mucyate bhayāt
vyādhito bandhanastho vā pravāseṣv atha parvasu
ṛtukāleṣu nārīṇāṃ bhrūṇahatyāṃ pramuñcati
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
veśanārīṣu vai jātāḥ parapreṣyāsu vā sutāḥ
nāradaḥ
kasya te bandhudāyādā bhavanti hi mahāmune
paṇyastrīṣu prasūtā ye yasya strī tasya te sutāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
krayāc ca kṛtrimāḥ putrāḥ pradānāc caiva datrimāḥ
paṇyanārīṣv aniyataḥ puṃso 'rtho vartate dhruvam
atra cāhitagarbhāyāḥ kasya putraṃ vadanti tam
nāradaḥ
tīrthabhūtāsu nārīṣu jñāyate yo 'bhigacchati
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
ṛtau tasya bhaved garbho yaṃ vā nārī na śaṅkate
narāṇāṃ tyajatāṃ bhāryāṃ kāmakrodhād guṇānvitām
nāradaḥ
aprasūtāṃ prasūtāṃ vā teṣāṃ pṛcchāmi niṣkṛtim
apāpāṃ tyajamānasya sādhvīṃ matvaivam āditaḥ
ātmavaṃśaḥ svadharmo vā tyajato niṣkṛtir na tu
yo naras tyajate bhāryāṃ puṣpiṇīm aprasūtikām
sa naṣṭavaṃśaḥ pitṛbhir yuktas tyajyeta daivataiḥ
bhāryām apatyasaṃjātāṃ prasūtāṃ putrapautriṇīm
putradāraparityāgī na saṃprāpnoti niṣkṛtim
evaṃ hi bhāryāṃ tyajatāṃ narāṇāṃ nāsti niṣkṛtiḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
nārhanti pramadās tyaktuṃ putrapautrapratiṣṭhitāḥ
kīdṛśīṃ saṃtyajan bhāryāṃ naro doṣair na lipyate
nāradaḥ
etad icchāmi tattvena vijñātum ṛṣisattama
mokṣadharmasthitānāṃ tu anyonyam anujānatām
bhāryāpatīnāṃ muktānām adharmo na vidhīyate
anyasaṅgāṃ gatāpatyāṃ śūdragāṃ paragāminīm
parīkṣya tyajamānānāṃ narāṇāṃ nāsti pātakam
pātake 'pi tu bhartavyau dvau tu mātā pitā tathā
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
bhāryāyāṃ vyabhicāriṇyāṃ narasya tyajato ruṣā
nāradaḥ
kathaṃ dharmo 'py adharmo vā bhavatīha mahāmate
anṛte 'pi hi satye vā yo nārīṃ dūṣitāṃ tyajet
arakṣamāṇaḥ svāṃ bhāryāṃ naro bhavati bhrūṇahā
apatyahetor yā nārī bhartāram atilaṅghayet
lolendriyeti sā rakṣyā na saṃtyājyā kathaṃ cana
nadyaś ca nāryaś ca samasvabhāvā
naitāḥ praduṣyanti narāvagāḍhāḥ
srotāṃsi nadyā vahate nipātaṃ
nārī rajobhiḥ punar eti śaucam
evaṃ nāryo na duṣyanti vyabhicāre 'pi bhartṛṇām
māsi māsi bhavet tyāgas tataḥ śuddhā bhavanty uta
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
kāni tīrthāni bhagavan nṛṇāṃ dehāśritāni vai
tāni vai śaṃsa bhagavan yāthātathyena pṛcchataḥ
sarvatīrtheṣu tīrthajña kiṃ tīrthaṃ paramaṃ nṛṇām
yatropaspṛśya pūtātmā naro bhavati nityaśaḥ
nāradaḥ
devarṣipitṛtīrthāni brāhmaṃ madhye 'tha vaiṣṇavam
nṛṇāṃ tīrthāni pañcāhuḥ pāṇau saṃnihitāni vai
ādyatīrthaṃ tu tīrthānāṃ vaiṣṇavo bhāga ucyate
yatropaspṛśya varṇānāṃ caturṇāṃ vardhate kulam
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
pitṛdaivatakāryāṇi vardhante pretya ceha ca
narāṇāṃ kāmavṛttānāṃ yā nāryo niravagrahāḥ
yāsām abhigraho nāsti tā me kathaya nārada
nāradaḥ
vāgur veśyā naṭī gopī tāntukī tunnavāyikī
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
nārī kirātī śabarī nartakī cānabhigrahā
etāsu jātā nārīṣu sarvavarṇeṣu ye sutāḥ
nāradaḥ
teṣu ke bandhudāyādā bhavanti ṛṣisattama
ya etāḥ parigṛhṇanti teṣām eva hi te sutāḥ
sarvatra tu pravṛttāsu bījaṃ naśyati dehinām
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
sarvastrīṣu pravṛttāś ca sādhuvedavivarjitāḥ
mānavāḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhāś ca vedamantrabahiṣkṛtāḥ
niyuktā havyakavyeṣu teṣāṃ dattaṃ kathaṃ bhavet
nāradaḥ
nārhanti havyakavyāni sāvitrīvarjitā dvijāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
vrātyeṣv annapradānaṃ tad yathā śūdreṣu vai tathā
dharmeṣv adhikṛtānāṃ tu narāṇāṃ muhyate manaḥ
nāradaḥ
kathaṃ nu vighno bhavati etad icchāmi veditum
arthāś ca nāryaś ca samānam etac
chreyāṃsi puṃsām iha mohayanti
ratipramodāt pramadā haranti
bhogair dhanaṃ cāpy upahanti dharmān
havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca dharmātmā sarvaṃ tac chrotriyo 'rhati
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
dattaṃ hi śrotriye sādhau jvalitāgnāv ivāhutiḥ
śrotriyāṇāṃ kule jātā vedārthaviditātmanām
nāradaḥ
hitvā kasmāt trayīṃ vidyāṃ vārtāṃ vṛttim upāśritāḥ
cāturvarṇyaṃ purā nyastaṃ suvidvatsu dvijātiṣu
tasmād varṇaiḥ saṃvibhajyā vṛttiḥ saṃkaravarjitā
ye cānye śrotriyāj jātāḥ saṃskṛtāḥ putragṛdhnubhiḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
pūrvanirvāṇanirvṛttāṃ jātāṃ vṛttim upāśritāḥ
nāradaḥ
asaṃskṛtāḥ śrotriyajāḥ saṃskṛtā jñāninaḥ katham
asaṃskāro vaidikaś ca na mānyaḥ śrotriyātmajaḥ
śuddhānvayaḥ śrotriyas tu sa vidvadbhiḥ samo 'nyathā
anadhīyānaputrāś ca vedasaṃskāravarjitāḥ
tasmāt te vedavijñāpi viprāḥ śrutinikāriṇaḥ
brahmarāśau purā sṛṣṭā vedasaṃskārasaṃskṛtāḥ
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
tasmāt teṣv eva te jātāḥ sādhavaḥ kuladhāriṇaḥ
svayaṃ krītāsu preṣyāsu prasūyante tu ye narāḥ
kasya nāryaḥ sutāś caiva bhavanti ṛṣisattama
nāradaḥ
svadāsyāṃ yo naro mohāt prasūyeta sa pāpakṛt
ihāpi ninditaḥ pretya apatyaṃ preṣyatāṃ nayet
sā tasya bhāryā putrā ye havyakavyapradās tu te
tasyā ye bāndhavāḥ ke cid viṣaktāḥ preṣyatāṃ gatāḥ
sarve tasyās tu saṃbandhān mucyante preṣyakarmasu
etat te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yad abhivyāhṛtaṃ tvayā
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
atha vā saṃśayaḥ kaś cid bhūyaḥ saṃpraṣṭum arhasi
amithyādarśano loke nāradaḥ sarvakovidaḥ
bhīṣmaḥ
pratyakṣadarśī lokānāṃ svayaṃbhūr iva sattamaḥ
iti saṃbhāṣya ṛṣibhir mārkaṇḍeyo mahātapāḥ
nāradaṃ cāpi satkṛtya tena caivābhisatkṛtaḥ
āmantrayitvā ṛṣibhiḥ prayayāv āśramaṃ muniḥ
ṛṣayaś cāpi tīrthānāṃ paricaryāṃ pracakramuḥ
sukṣetrabījasaṃskāraviśuddho brahmacaryayā
nityanaimittikāt snāto manaḥśuddhyā ca śudhyati
bhīṣmaḥ
brāhmaṇāḥ pātrabhūtāś ca śuddhā naivaṃ pitṛṣv iha
aśvamedhasahasrād dhi satyam eva viśiṣyate
tṛpte tṛptāḥ sarvadevāḥ pitaro munayo 'pi ca
rājapreṣyo 'pi sāvitrīṃ japan nityaṃ samāhitaḥ
mātā pātraṃ pitā pātraṃ pātraṃ kṣīṇāś ca bāndhavāḥ
dīnaḥ pātraṃ vratī pātraṃ pātraṃ vṛttair alaṃkṛtam
muhūrtānāṃ trayaṃ pūrvam ahnaḥ prātar iti smṛtam
japadhyānādibhis tasmin vipraiḥ kāryaṃ śubhavratam
saṃgavākhyaṃ tribhāgaṃ tu madhyāhnas trimuhūrtakaḥ
laukikaṃ saṃgave 'rdhaṃ ca snānādi hy atha madhyame
caturtham aparāhṇaṃ tu trimuhūrtaṃ tu pitryakam
sāyāhnas trimuhūrtaṃ ca madhyamaṃ kavibhiḥ smṛtam
caturthe tv aparāhṇākhye śrāddhaṃ kuryāt sadā nṛpa
prāgudīcīmukhā viprā viśvedevāś ca dakṣiṇāḥ
śrāviteṣu sutṛpteṣu piṇḍaṃ dadyāt sadakṣiṇam
gohiṃsāyāṃ caturbhāgaṃ pūrvaṃ viprādiketanaḥ
varṇāvareṣu bhuñjānaṃ kramāc chūdre caturguṇam
nānyatra brāhmaṇo brūyāt pūrvaṃ vipreṇa ketitaḥ
abhojane ca doṣaḥ syād varjayec chūdraketanam
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭāṅgo dvijo nordhvāṃ gatiṃ labhet
aśucir naiva cāśnīyān nāstiko mānavarjitaḥ
na pūrvaṃ laṅghayel lobhād ekavarṇo 'pi pārthiva
viprāḥ smṛtā bhūmidevā upakurvāṇavarjitāḥ
pālāśo dvijadaṇḍaḥ syād aśvatthaḥ kṣatriyasya tu
audumbaraś ca vaiśyasya dharma eṣa yudhiṣṭhira
vratānāṃ pāraṇārthāya gurvarthe yajñadakṣiṇām
niveśārthaṃ ca vidvāṃsas teṣāṃ dattaṃ mahāphalam
pitroś ca rakṣaṇārthāya putradārārtham eva ca
mahāvyādhivimokṣārthaṃ teṣu dattaṃ mahāphalam
bālāḥ striyaś ca vāñchanti subhaktaṃ cāpy asādhanāḥ
svargam āyānti dattvaiṣāṃ nirayān nopayānti te
viṣamavyavahārāś ca viṣamāś caiva vṛddhiṣu
lābheṣu viṣamāś caiva te vai nirayagāminaḥ
dyūtasaṃvyavahārāś ca niṣparīkṣāś ca mānavāḥ
prāṇihiṃsāpravṛttāś ca te vai nirayagāminaḥ
saṃśrutya cāpradātāro daridrāṇāṃ vinindakāḥ
śrotriyāṇāṃ vinītānāṃ daridrāṇāṃ vinindakāḥ
kṣamiṇāṃ nindakāś caiva te vai nirayagāminaḥ
bhakṣyapānānnadātāras tathā pādukadā narāḥ
ye vai svaśaktitaḥ kuryus te vai nirayaśatravaḥ
gaṅgāditīrthaniratāḥ pitṛtīrthakarāś ca ye
iṣṭāpūrtaratā ye ca te narāḥ svargagāminaḥ
jātito brāhmaṇānāṃ hi mahāparibhavāt tathā
tadvṛtticchedanaṃ caiva taddāraparibhāvanāt
brāhmaṇena ca tat kuryāt taṃ vidyād brahmaghātakam
sādhvācāreṣu vipreṣu sāsūyo nikṛtiḥ śaṭhaḥ
sindhum āsādya vimalāṃ vigāhya svargam āpnuyāt
na kva cid bhayam āpnoti sarvatra labhate śubham
tṛtīyaṃ parvataṃ krauñcaṃ śudhyate brahmahatyayā
ekamāsaṃ nirāhāro vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet
agnikanyāpure vaset
karavīrapure snātvā
divaṃ vrajen mahābāho sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate
kālodakaṃ nandikuṇḍaṃ tathaivottaramānasam
abhyetya yojanaśatād bhrūṇahā vipramucyate
nandīśvarasya mūrtiṃ tu dṛṣṭvā mucyeta kilbiṣaiḥ
svargamārge naraḥ snātvā svargalokaṃ sa gacchati
darśanād gamanāt pūto bhaved anaśanād api
uvāsa rajanīṃ tatra susukhaṃ sukhabhāg ṛṣiḥ
śilavṛttis tu yat kṛtyaṃ prātas tat kṛtavāñ śuciḥ
siddha uvāca
puṣkaraṃ ca prabhāsaṃ ca naimiṣaṃ himavān giriḥ
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva vedikā mathurā gayā
sarasvatyāruṇā caiva kurukṣetraṃ pṛthūdakam
śilavṛttir uvāca
eteṣāṃ katamasyāhaṃ māhātmyaṃ kathayāmi te
bālyāt prabhṛti viprendra bhaktiḥ kautukam eva ca
māhātmyaṃ prati viprendra gaṅgāyā mama tattvataḥ
tyaktāni yāni vai yeṣāṃ tyāgāt svargo vidhīyate
ye vā yathāvidhi snātā gaṅgāyāṃ sāghamarṣaṇāḥ
yuktāś ca pātakais tyaktvā dehaṃ śuddhā bhavanti te
mucyante dehasaṃtyāgād gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame
anye ca devā munayaḥ pretāni pitṛbhiḥ saha
tarpitās tṛptim āyānti triṣu lokeṣu sarvaśaḥ
saṃvatsaraṃ jale vāsaṃ gaṅgāyāṃ tatsamaṃ matam
gaṅgā gaṅgeti yo brūyād yojanānāṃ śatair api
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati
prāyaścittāni dīyante yatra gaṅgā na vidyate
gaṅgā ca vidyate yatra prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet
darśanāj jāyate śuddhiḥ snānena paramā gatiḥ
dhanyā gaṅgā kanakhale kurukṣetre sarasvatī
grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā
gaṅgām upāśritā ye vai śivās te 'pi bhavanty api
upajīvyā yathā dhenur lokānāṃ brāhmam eva vā
haviṣāṃ ca yathā somas taraṇeṣu tathākṣayam
tathā spṛśan so 'sya pāpaṃ sarvam eva prakarṣati
utpādayati gaṅgā vai prītiṃ nityasukhapradām
tattīragānāṃ tapasā śrāddhapārāyaṇādibhiḥ
gaṅgādvāraprabhṛtibhis tattīrthair na paraṃ nṛṇām
sāyaṃ prātaḥ smared gaṅgāṃ nityaṃ snāne tu kīrtayet
tarpaṇe pitṛpūjāsu maraṇe cāpi saṃsmaret
durmṛtān anapatyāṃś ca sā mṛtān anayad divam
prātas trimārgeṣu kṛtāvagāhā
vipāpmanāṃ vitatā viśvagoptā
nārāyaṇād akṣayāt pūrvajātā
viṣṇoḥ padāc chiṃśumārād dhruvāc ca
somāt sūryān merurūpāc ca viṣṇoḥ
samāgatā śivamūrdhno himādrim
sevyā devī sarvabhāvena puṇyā
yā śaṃkareṇa bhuvanatrayaśaṃkareṇa
viṣṇoḥ pādakṣāḷinī jahnukanyā
bhavān viśiṣṭo buddhyā ca prajñayā tapasā tathā
sarveṣāṃ caiva jātānāṃ satām etan na saṃśayaḥ
nānyas tvad anyo lokeṣu praṣṭavyo 'sti janādhipa
tvarayā taṃ kharair yuktam āsthāya ratham āvrajat
mataṅga durlabham idaṃ vipratvaṃ prārthyate tvayā
vinaśiṣyasi durbuddhe tad upārama māciram
brāhmaṇyaṃ durlabhaṃ tāta prārthayāno na lapsyase
na hi śakyaṃ tvayā prāptuṃ brāhmaṇyam iha durmate
sarvastrīṣu pravṛttāś ca ye ca vedabahiṣkṛtāḥ
kāṇḍapṛṣṭhās tu te jñeyāḥ sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtāḥ
evam ukto mataṅgas tu saṃśitātmā yatavrataḥ
sahasram ekapādena tato 'bdānām atiṣṭhata
taṃ sahasrāvare kāle śakro draṣṭum upāgamat
mataṅga uvāca
tad eva ca punar vākyam uvāca balavṛtrahā
idaṃ varṣasahasraṃ vai brahmacārī samāhitaḥ
śakra uvāca
atiṣṭham ekapādena brāhmaṇyaṃ nāpnuyāṃ katham
caṇḍālayonau jātena nāvāpyaṃ vai kathaṃ cana
anyaṃ kāmaṃ vṛṇīṣva tvaṃ mā vṛthā te 'stv ayaṃ śramaḥ
viruddham iha dṛśyate
brāhmaṇyaṃ durlabhataraṃ
tad utsṛjyeha duṣprāpaṃ brāhmaṇyam akṛtātmabhiḥ
anyaṃ varaṃ vṛṇīṣva tvaṃ durlabho 'yaṃ hi te varaḥ
daivaṃ tu nūnam etad vai yad ahaṃ mātṛdoṣataḥ
etām avasthāṃ saṃprāpto dharmajñaḥ san puraṃdara
nūnaṃ daivaṃ na śakyaṃ hi pauruṣeṇātivartitum
yad ahaṃ yatnavān evaṃ na labhe vipratāṃ vibho
evaṃ gate tu dharmajña dātum arhasi me varam
yadi te 'ham anugrāhyaḥ kiṃ cid vā sukṛtaṃ mama
bhīṣma uvāca
vṛṇīṣveti tadā prāha tatas taṃ balavṛtrahā
coditas tu mahendreṇa mataṅgaḥ prābravīd idam
evaṃ varaṃ vṛṇe deva sa me sidhyatu vāsava
kartum arhasi tad deva śirasā tvāṃ prasādaye
mataṅga gamyatāṃ śīghram evam etad bhaviṣyati
striyaś ca sarvās tvā loke yakṣyante bhūtikarmaṇi
evaṃ tavākṣayā kīrtir bhaviṣyati tapodhana
kīrtiś ca te 'tulā vatsa triṣu lokeṣu yāsyati
martyānāṃ bharatarṣabha
brāhmaṇyaṃ nāma duṣprāpam indreṇoktaṃ mahātmanā
viśvāmitro mahārāja rājā brāhmaṇatāṃ gataḥ
kathitaṃ bhavatā sarvaṃ vistareṇa pitāmaha
tac ca rājan mayā sarvaṃ śrutaṃ buddhimatāṃ vara
āgamo hi paro 'smākaṃ tvattaḥ kauravanandana
vītahavyas tu rājarṣir viśruto vai pitāmaha
brāhmaṇatvam anuprāpta iti rājan mahāyaśāḥ
yuddhe vinirjito rājan gaṅgāyamunayor anu
prapadya śaraṇaṃ tasya mūrdhnā ca nipapāta ha
uvāca bhagavantaṃ taṃ putraṃ mānyaṃ bṛhaspateḥ
tam uvāca bharadvājo jyeṣṭhaḥ putro bṛhaspateḥ
purodhāḥ śīlasaṃpanno divodāsaṃ mahīpatim
kim āgamanakṛtyaṃ te sarvaṃ prabrūhi me 'nagha
yat te priyaṃ tat kariṣye na me 'trāsti vicāraṇā
purodhās tasya rājendra divodāsasya dhīmataḥ
stūyamānaḥ surarṣibhiḥ
bandibhir vandyamānaś ca babhau sūrya ivoditaḥ
sa rathī baddhanistriṃśo
āsanaṃ śiṣyamadhye ca bhṛgur anyat samādiśat
tam abhyanandad bhagavān satkāreṇa svayaṃ bhṛguḥ
uvāca cainaṃ bhagavān kiṃ kāryam iti satkṛtam
tam uvāca tato rājā daivodāsiḥ pratardanaḥ
ṛṣer hi vacanaṃ satyaṃ sarve syur brāhmaṇā iti
avandhyakālā ye 'lubdhās trivarge sādhaneṣu vā
viśiṣṭācārayuktāś ca nārāyaṇa namāmi tān
yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
pitāmaha mahāprājña sarvaśāstraviśārada
tvatto 'haṃ śrotum icchāmi dharmaṃ bharatasattama
śaraṇāgataṃ ye rakṣanti bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham
kiṃ tasya bharataśreṣṭha phalaṃ bhavati tattvataḥ
bhīṣmaḥ
idaṃ śṛṇu mahārāja dharmaputra mahāyaśaḥ
itihāsaṃ purāvṛttaṃ śaraṇārthaṃ mahāphalam
prapātyamānaḥ śyenena kapotaḥ priyadarśanaḥ
vṛṣadarbhaṃ mahābhāgaṃ narendraṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ
sa taṃ dṛṣṭvā viśuddhātmā trāsād aṅkam upāgatam
āśvāsyāśvasihīty āha na te 'sti bhayam aṇḍaja
bhayaṃ te sumahat kasmāt kutra kiṃ vā kṛtaṃ tvayā
yena tvam iha saṃprāpto visaṃjño bhrāntacetanaḥ
navanīlotpalāpīḍa cāruvarṇa sudarśana
dāḍimāśokapuṣpākṣa mā trasasvābhayaṃ tava
matsakāśam anuprāptaṃ na tvāṃ kaś cit samutsahet
manasā grahaṇaṃ kartuṃ rakṣādhyakṣapuraskṛtam
kāśirājyaṃ tavādyedaṃ tvadarthaṃ jīvitaṃ tathā
śyenaḥ
tyajeyaṃ bhava viśrabdhaḥ kapota na bhayaṃ tava
mamaitad vihitaṃ bhakṣyaṃ na rājaṃs trātum arhasi
atikrāntaṃ ca prāptaṃ ca prayatnāc copapāditam
māṃsaṃ ca rudhiraṃ cāsya majjā medaś ca me hitam
paritoṣakaro hy eṣa mama māsyāgrato bhava
tṛṣṇā me bādhate 'tyugrā kṣudhā nirdahatīva mām
muñcainaṃ na hi śakṣyāmi rājan mandayituṃ kṣudhām
mayā hy anusṛto hy eṣa matpakṣanakhavikṣataḥ
kiṃ cid ucchvāsaniḥśvāsaṃ na rājan goptum arhasi
yadi svaviṣaye rājan prabhus tvaṃ rakṣaṇe nṛṇām
khecarasya tṛṣārtasya na tvaṃ prabhur atho mama
yadi vairiṣu bhṛtyeṣu svajanavyavahārayoḥ
viṣayeṣv indriyāṇāṃ ca ākāśe mā parākrama
prabhutvaṃ hi parākramya samyak pakṣahareṣu te
bhīṣmaḥ
yadi tvam iha dharmārthī mām api draṣṭum arhasi
śrutvā śyenasya tad vākyaṃ rājarṣir vismayaṃ gataḥ
saṃbhāvya cainaṃ tad vākyaṃ tadarthī pratyabhāṣata
govṛṣo vā varāho vā mṛgo vā mahiṣo 'pi vā
tvadartham adya kriyatāṃ kṣudhāpraśamanāya te
śaraṇāgataṃ na tyajeyam iti me vratam āhitam
śyenaḥ
na muñcati mamāṅgāni dvijo 'yaṃ paśya vai dvija
na varāhaṃ na cokṣāṇaṃ na cānyān vividhān dvijān
bhakṣayāmi mahārāja kim annādyena tena me
yas tu me vihito bhakṣaḥ svayaṃ devaiḥ sanātanaḥ
śyenāḥ kapotān khādanti sthitir eṣā sanātanī
uśīnara kapote tu yadi snehas tavānagha
rājā
tatas tvaṃ me prayacchādya svamāṃsaṃ tulayā dhṛtam
mahān anugraho me 'dya yas tvam evam ihāttha mām
bāḍham evaṃ kariṣyāmīty uktvāsau rājasattamaḥ
utkṛtyotkṛtya māṃsāni tulayā samatolayat
antaḥpure tatas tasya striyo ratnavibhūṣitāḥ
hāhābhūtā viniṣkrāntāḥ śrutvā paramaduḥkhitāḥ
tāsāṃ ruditaśabdena mantribhṛtyajanasya ca
babhūva sumahān nādo meghagambhīranisvanaḥ
niruddhaṃ gaganaṃ sarvaṃ śubhraṃ meghaiḥ samantataḥ
mahī pracalitā cāsīt tasya satyena karmaṇā
sa rājā pārśvataś caiva bāhubhyām ūrutaś ca yat
tāni māṃsāni saṃchidya tulāṃ pūrayate śanaiḥ
tathāpi na samas tena kapotena babhūva ha
asthibhūto yadā rājā nirmāṃso rudhirasravaḥ
tulāṃ tataḥ samārūḍhaḥ svaṃ māṃsakṣayam utsṛjan
tataḥ sendrās trayo lokās taṃ narendram upasthitāḥ
bheryaś cākāśagais tatra vāditā devaduṃdubhiḥ
amṛtenāvasiktaś ca vṛṣadarbho nareśvaraḥ
divyaiś ca susukhair mālyair abhivṛṣṭaḥ punaḥ punaḥ
devagandharvasaṃghātair apsarobhiś ca sarvataḥ
nṛttaś caivopagītaś ca pitāmaha iva prabhuḥ
hemaprāsādasaṃbādhaṃ maṇikāñcanatoraṇam
sa vaidūryamaṇistambhaṃ vimānaṃ samadhiṣṭhitaḥ
sa rājarṣir gataḥ svargaṃ karmaṇā tena śāśvatam
śaraṇāgateṣu caivaṃ tvaṃ kuru sarvaṃ yudhiṣṭhira
bhaktānām anuraktānām āśritānāṃ ca rakṣitā
dayāvān sarvabhūteṣu paratra sukham edhate
sādhuvṛtto hi yo rājā sadvṛttam anutiṣṭhati
kiṃ na prāptaṃ bhavet tena svavyājeneha karmaṇā
sa rājarṣir viśuddhātmā dhīraḥ satyaparākramaḥ
kāśīnām īśvaraḥ khyātas triṣu lokeṣu karmaṇā
yo 'py anyaḥ kārayed evaṃ śaraṇāgatarakṣaṇam
so 'pi gaccheta tām eva gatiṃ bharatasattama
idaṃ vṛttaṃ hi rājarṣer vṛṣadarbhasya kīrtayan
pūtātmā vai bhavel loke śṛṇuyād yaś ca nityaśaḥ
tapasvinaś ca ye nityaṃ kaumārabrahmacāriṇaḥ
tapasā bhāvitātmāno durgāṇy atitaranti te
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ cārcane ratāḥ
śiṣṭānnabhojino ye ca durgāṇy atitaranti te
brāhmaṇānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ ca pūjā ca sukham icchataḥ
kartavyaṃ pārthiveneha tad viddhi bharatarṣabha
mānyās teṣāṃ sādhavo ye na nindyāś cāpy asādhavaḥ
satyaṃ bravīmi te rājan vinaśyeyur asaṃśayam
iti māṃ nāradaḥ prāha satataṃ sarvabhūtaye
prayato bharatarṣabha
brāhmaṇāñ śrutasaṃpannān
sarvān ete haniṣyanti brāhmaṇā jātamanyavaḥ
yuṣmatsaṃmānanāṃ prītiṃ pāvanaiḥ kṣatriyāśramam
amutreha samāyānti vaiśyaśūdrādikās tathā
arakṣitāś ca yuṣmābhir viruddhā yānti viplavam
yuṣmattejodhṛtā lokās tad rakṣatha jagattrayam
sa pṛṣṭas tān uvācedaṃ siddhāṃs tatra niśākaraḥ
vidyāvido lokavidas tapodamasamanvitāḥ
nityaṃ pūjyāś ca vandyāś ca dvijā lokadvayecchubhiḥ
vidyātapobhyāṃ hīnena na tu grāhyaḥ pratigrahaḥ
gṛhṇan pratigraham atho nayaty ātmānam eva ca
pratigrahasamartho 'pi nādatte yaḥ pratigraham
ye lokā dānaśīlānāṃ sa tān āpnoti puṣkalān
pātrāṇām uttamaṃ pātraṃ tad āhur dharmacintakāḥ
duṣṭācārāḥ pāparatā asatyā māyayā vṛtāḥ
adṛṣṭabuddhibahulāḥ prāyeṇety avagamyatām
suhṛdy avadyahṛdyās tā vairakopālayāḥ sma tāḥ
susnātaṃ puruṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā sucandanalavokṣitam
yoniḥ svadyati nārīṇāṃ dṛteḥ pādād ivodakam
yadi jihvāsahasraṃ syāj jīvec ca śaradāṃ śatam
ananyakarmā strīdoṣān anuktvā nidhanaṃ vrajet
doṣāspade 'śucau deśe hy āsāṃ raktās tv aho narāḥ
imāḥ prajā mahābāho dhārmikya iti naḥ śrutam
satkṛtāsatkṛtāś cāpi vikurvanti manaḥ sadā
dvijānāṃ ca gurūṇāṃ ca mahāgurunṛpādinām
kṣaṇastrīsaṅgakāmotthā yātanāho nirantarā
araktamanasāṃ nityaṃ brahmacaryamalātmanām
tapodamārcanādhyānayuktānāṃ śuddhir uttamā
tan nibodha mahārāja kathayāmi samāsataḥ
sā tvayā satataṃ rakṣyā ity uktvā prayayau muniḥ
mana eva manuṣyāṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ bandhamokṣayoḥ
anyathāliṅgyate kāntā duhitāliṅgyate 'nyathā
garbhasaṃstho yathā putro jāyamāno na lipyate
evaṃ jñānī na laukyaiś ca karmabhir naiva duṣyate
vipulo 'tha ruciṃ tasmin yogāt tāṃ praviveśa ha
rakṣituṃ gurupatnīṃ sa bahurūpāc chatakratoḥ
devaśarmā mahāmatiḥ
sthitiṃ ca dharme jagrāha
gaccha gaccheti puṣpārtham āśramaṃ me 'tha bhārata
tathaiva hi bhaveyus te lokāḥ pāpakṛto yathā
kṛtvā nācakṣataḥ karma mama tac ca tvayā kṛtam
tat tvayā vai tathā kāryam abhicārabhayātmakam
manodoṣavihīnānāṃ na doṣaḥ syāt tathā tava
anyathāliṅgyate kāntā snehena duhitānyathā
yateś ca kāmukānāṃ ca yoṣidrūpe 'nyathā matiḥ
aśikṣayaiva manasaḥ prāyo lokas tu vañcyate
lolety udvijate loko vaktrāsava iti spṛhā
abandhāyogyamanasām iti mantrātmadaivatam
na rāgasnehalobhāndhaṃ karmiṇāṃ tan mahāphalam
niṣkaṣāyo viśuddhas tvaṃ rucyāveśān na dūṣitaḥ
anṛtāv iha parvādidoṣavarjaṃ narādhipa
anṛtān api parvādidoṣavarjī nirāmayaḥ
suptāṃ mattāṃ pramattāṃ vā raho yatropagacchati
sa pāpiṣṭho vivāhānāṃ paiśācaḥ prathito 'dhamaḥ
nātibālāṃ vahanty anye anityatvāt prajārthinaḥ
vahanti karmiṇas tasyām antaḥśuddhivyapekṣayā
aparānvayasaṃbhūtāṃ saṃsvapnādivivarjitām
kāmo yasyāṃ niṣiddhaś ca ke cid icchanti cāpadi
sā praśastā dvijātīnāṃ dārakarmaṇi maithune
striyo yatra ca pūjyante ramante tatra devatāḥ
smṛta ekaś caturbhāgaḥ kanyābhāgas tu dharmataḥ
abhrātṛkā samagrārhā cārthasyety apare viduḥ
vaiśyāputro 'pi bhūyāṃś ca śūdrāputrān na saṃśayaḥ
bhayaṃ saṃjanayaty eva tasmād deyadhanas tv asau
caityadrumasmaśāneṣu śaileṣūpavaneṣu ca
kārṣṇāyasam alaṃkāraṃ parigṛhya ca nityaśaḥ
vaseyur ete pitryais tu vartayantaḥ svakarmabhiḥ
ātmānam ātmanākhyāti karmasvaiḥ karmabhir naraḥ
svaśīlacāritrakṛtaiḥ karmair vāpi śubhāśubhaiḥ
bhīṣmaḥ
ātmā putras tu vijñeyaḥ prathamo bahudhā pare
sve kṣetre saṃskṛte yas tu putram utpādayet svayam
tam aurasaṃ vijānīyāt putraṃ prathamakalpitam
agniṃ prajāpatiṃ ceṣṭvā varāya pratipāditā
putrikā syād duhitari saṃkalpenāpi vā sutaḥ
talpe jātaḥ pramītasya klībasya patitasya vā
svadharmeṇa niyukto yaḥ sa putraḥ kṣetrajaḥ smṛtaḥ
mātā pitā ca dadyātāṃ yam adbhiḥ putram āpadi
sadṛśaṃ prītisaṃyuktau vijñeyo dāttakaḥ sutaḥ
sadṛśaṃ tu prakuryād yaṃ guṇadoṣavicakṣaṇam
putraṃ putraguṇair yuktaṃ vijñeyas tu sa kṛtrimaḥ
utpadyate yas tu gūḍhaṃ na ca jñāyeta kasya cit
sa bhaved gūḍhajo nāma tasya syād yasya talpajaḥ
mātāpitṛbhyām utsṛṣṭaṃ tayor anyatareṇa vā
yaḥ putraṃ pratigṛhṇīyād apaviddhaḥ sa ucyate
pitṛveśmani kanyā tu yaṃ putraṃ janayed rahaḥ
kānīnaṃ taṃ vaden nāmnā voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavaḥ
yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate jñātājñātāpi vā satī
voḍhuḥ sa garbho bhavati sahoḍha iti cocyate
yā patyā vā parityaktā atha vā svecchayāpi vā
utpādayed akṣatāyāṃ sa paunarbhava ucyate
mātāpitṛvihīno vā sakto vā syād akāraṇāt
ātmānaṃ sparśayed yas tu svayaṃdattaḥ sa ucyate
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthaṃ mātāpitror yam antikāt
sa sutas tasya sadṛśaḥ krīto nāma budhaiḥ smṛtaḥ
dvādaśaite pare prāhur evaṃ dharmasya pāṭhakāḥ
sā ced akṣatayoniḥ syād gatapratyāgatāpi vā
paunarbhavena bhartrā sā punaḥ saṃskāram arhati
yaṃ brāhmaṇas tu śūdrāyāṃ kāmād utpādayet sutam
sa pārayann eva śavas tasmāt pāraśavaḥ smṛtaḥ
dāsyāṃ vā dāsadāsyāṃ vā yaḥ śūdrasya suto bhavet
so 'nujñāto hared aṃśam iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ
kṣetrajādīn sutān etān ekādaśa yathoditān
putrapratinidhīn āhuḥ kriyālopān manīṣiṇaḥ
bhrātṝṇām ekajātānām ekaś cet putravān bhavet
sarvāṃs tāṃs tena putreṇa putriṇo manur abravīt
sarvāsām ekapatnīnām ekā cet putriṇī bhavet
sarvās tās tena putreṇa prāha putravatīr manuḥ
saṃskṛtasya satas tasya varṇagotraṃ bhavaty uta
dīyatāṃ te purohita
avilambitam ity evam ṛṣiḥ kaṣṭāt pramucyatām
gavijātaḥ
brāhmaṇānāṃ gavāṃ caiva kulam ekaṃ dvidhā kṛtam
ekatra mantrās tiṣṭhanti havir anyatra tiṣṭhati
atyantāpadi śaktānāṃ paritrāṇaṃ hi kurvatām
yā gatir viditā tv adya narake śaraṇaṃ bhavān
ārohaṇaṃ niṣādānāṃ matsyānāṃ caiva bhārata
tvayāpi gauḥ pradātavyā sālaṃkārā bhaven nṛpa
brāhme bale supūrṇānām eteṣāṃ cyavanādinām
dvāviṃśena samutthāya niścakrāma tapodhanaḥ
athāha bhagavāṃs tau tu pratibuddho viśāṃ pate
tailābhyaṅgaṃ prayacchetāṃ snātum icchāmi pārthiva
brāhmaṇā eva jāyeran nānyo varṇaḥ kathaṃ cana
īdṛśaṃ ratham āruhya ko 'nyo jīvitum utsahet
brāhmaṇyaṃ durlabhaṃ loke tal labdhvā durlabhaṃ tapaḥ
siddhis tatrāpi duṣprāpā siddher api parā gatiḥ
rathena vāhitaś cāpi śramāt krodhodbhavāya te
jamadagnau mahābhāga tapasā bhāvitātmani
sa cāpi bhṛguśārdūlas taṃ vedaṃ dhārayiṣyati
kṣatrahantā bhaved dhiṃsram iti devaṃ sanātanam
nārāyaṇam upāsyāsya varāt taṃ putram icchati
labheta kāmaṃ sa bhaved arogas
tathā surūpaś ca narendraloke
pustakaṃ ca tathā gāvaḥ kanyā dānaṃ tathaiva ca
ye na dāsyanti puruṣās teṣāṃ saukhyaṃ pareṇa kim
apūjyamānāḥ kauravya pūjārhās te tathāvidhāḥ
yat kiṃ cit pāṇḍaraṃ puṣpaṃ yat kiṃ cid anulepanam
alakṣmīparihārāya nityaṃ deyaṃ yudhiṣṭhira
nityānuṣṭhānayuktāś cet pratigṛhṇanti sādhavaḥ
yājakā dvijasattamāḥ
dhane satyapradātṝṇāṃ
vidvadbhyaḥ saṃpradānena tatrāpy aṃśo 'sya pūjayā
yajvabhyaś cātha vidvadbhyo dattvā lokaṃ pradāpayet
pradadyāj jñānadātṝṇāṃ jñānadānāṃśabhāg bhavet
tadarthaṃ jīvitaṃ te 'stu mā tebhyo 'pratipālanam
anartho brāhmaṇasyaiṣa yad vittanicayo mahān
śriyā hy abhīkṣṇaṃ saṃvāso darpayet saṃpramohayet
brāhmaṇeṣu pramūḍheṣu dharmo vipraṇaśed dhruvam
dharmapraṇāśe bhūtānām abhāvaḥ syān na saṃśayaḥ
yo rakṣibhyaḥ saṃpradāya rājā rāṣṭraṃ vilumpati
yakṣye rāṣṭrād dhanaṃ tasmād ānayadhvam iti bruvan
yac cādāya tad ājñaptaṃ bhītaṃ dattaṃ sudāruṇam
yajed rājā na taṃ yajñaṃ praśaṃsanty asya sādhavaḥ
apīḍitāḥ susaṃvṛddhā ye dadaty anukūlataḥ
tādṛśenābhyupāyena yaṣṭavyaṃ nodyamāhṛtaiḥ
yadā pariniṣicyeta nihito vai yathāvidhi
tadā rājā mahāyajñair yajeta bahudakṣiṇaiḥ
vṛddhabāladhanaṃ rakṣyam andhasya kṛpaṇasya ca
na khātapūrvaṃ kurvīta na rudantīdhanaṃ haret
hṛtaṃ kṛpaṇavittaṃ hi rāṣṭraṃ hanti nṛpaśriyam
dadyāc ca mahato bhogān kṣudbhayaṃ praṇudet satām
yeṣāṃ svādūni bhojyāni samavekṣyanti bālakāḥ
nāśnanti vidhivat tāni kiṃ nu pāpataraṃ tataḥ
yadi te tādṛśo rāṣṭre vidvān sīdet kṣudhā dvijaḥ
bhrūṇahatyāṃ ca gacchethāḥ kṛtvā pāpam ivottamam
dhik tasya jīvitaṃ rājño rāṣṭre yasyāvasīdati
dvijo 'nyo vā manuṣyo 'pi śibir āha vaco yathā
yasya sma viṣaye rājñaḥ snātakaḥ sīdati kṣudhā
avṛddhim eti tad rāṣṭraṃ vindate saha rājakam
krośantyo yasya vai rāṣṭrād dhriyante tarasā striyaḥ
krośatāṃ patiputrāṇāṃ mṛto 'sau na sa jīvati
ya etāṃ viduṣe dadyāt pṛthivīṃ pṛthivīpatiḥ
pṛthivyām etad iṣṭaṃ sa rājyād rājyam iti vrajet
punaś ca jananaṃ prāpya rājaiva syān na saṃśayaḥ
ekāhārakarīṃ dattvā ṣaṣṭisāhasram ūrdhvagaḥ
tāvaty āharaṇe hṛtvā narakaṃ dviguṇottaram
daśahastena vaṃśena daśavaṃśān samantataḥ
pañcahastādhikaṃ dadyād etad gocarma ucyate
sauvarṇā yatra prāsādā vasor dhārāś ca kāmadāḥ
gandharvāpsaraso yatra tatra gacchanti bhūmidāḥ
vidyādānaṃ ca kanyānāṃ dānaṃ pāpaharaṃ param
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā rāṣṭraghāte 'tha svāminaḥ
kulastrīṇāṃ paribhave mṛtās te bhūmidaiḥ saha
svanirjitāṃ svayaṃkrītāṃ bhṛtāṃ ca vārpayen nṛpa
sunirmitāṃ suvikrītāṃ subhṛtāṃ vāpi yan nṛpaḥ
hṛte kṣetre daridrāṇām aśrupātaḥ sudāruṇaḥ
iṣṭāpūrtaṃ narasyāsya hanyāt tripuruṣaṃ kulam
kadalīvanasāmrādyaiḥ supūrṇāṃ sparśayen mahīm
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyaḥ svargaloke mahīyate
brahmaloke dharāpradam
upatiṣṭhanti bhūtāni
āśayābhyāgataṃ vipraṃ bhojanārtham upasthitam
tāmrāṇi tilapātrāṇi triṃśatpalamitāni tu
tāṃ prāpnuvanti gāṃ dattvā vimuktāḥ sarvakilbiṣaiḥ
gorasānāṃ na vikretur apañcayajanasya ca
vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tu tilān dadyād dvijātiṣu
teṣāṃ māghyāṃ pradānena sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt
pānīyābhyarthinaṃ dṛṣṭvā prītyā dadyāt tvarānvitaḥ
vastre tantupramāṇena dīpe nimiṣavatsaram
gavāṃ lomapramāṇena svargabhogam upāśnute
jale bindupramāṇena tad etāny upavartaya
saṃtāḍyā na tu pādena gavāṃ madhye ca na vrajet
juhoti yad bhojayati yad dadāti gavāṃ rasaiḥ
arthasya daśabhāg dātā tan nivṛttapravṛttaye
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
brāhmaṇasvāpahāreṇa kiyān doṣo bhavaty uta
brāhmasvam ucyate kiṃ vā tan me brūhi pitāmaha
vipradāre parahṛte viprasvanicaye tathā
paritrāyanti śaktās tu namas tebhyo mṛtās tu vā
na pālayanti nihatā ye tān vaivasvato yamaḥ
daṇḍayan bhartsayan nityaṃ nirayebhyo na muñcati
tathā gavāṃ paritrāṇe pīḍane ca śubhāśubhe
vipragoṣu viśeṣeṇa rakṣiteṣu gṛheṣu vā
na mṛtaḥ svargam āpnoti putrapautrasamanvitaḥ
kṣīrasravā vai sarito girīṃś ca sarpiṣāṃ tathā
vimalaṃ caiva toyaṃ ca sarvaṃ svādu tathaiva ca
gā vai dadyān nyāyabhūtā dvijebhyo
yo yātum ākāṅkṣati devalokam
rājasūyāśvamedhābhyāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ
kāntāraṃ yaḥ prapannas tu gāṃ dvijāya prayacchati
kṣemeṇa pratimucyeta tam adhvānaṃ na saṃśayaḥ
yānapātraṃ samārohan yaś ca gāṃ saṃprayacchati
tīrtvā samudraṃ kṣemeṇa pratyāyāti gṛhaṃ gṛhī
mṛtyukāle sahasrākṣa yaś ca gāṃ saṃprayacchati
mṛtyuṃ sukhaṃ sa labhate lokāṃś cāpnoty anuttamān
kṣatriyo yaś ca vai kaś cid yātrākāle prayacchati
gobhiś ca samanudhyāto jitvā śatrūn mahīyate
yaḥ kaś cit karma yat kiṃ cit kartum ārabhate nṛṣu
gāvo dattvā sa tat sarvaṃ sukhopāyena vindati
yaṃ yaṃ kāmaṃ samuddiśya gāṃ dvijāya prayacchati
tasya saṃpatsyate kāmaḥ paratra ca śubhāṃ gatim
dānād damo viśiṣṭo hi dānaṃ kiṃ cid dvijātaye
vidyādānāt paraṃ nāsti vedavidyā mahāphalā
śūrāṇāṃ cāpi nirdiṣṭaṃ phalaṃ ca niyataṃ śṛṇu
tataḥ prajāsu sṛṣṭāsu dakṣādyaiḥ kṣudhitāḥ prajāḥ
prajāpatim upādhāvan viniścitya caturmukham
mukhajā sāsṛjad dhātuḥ surabhiṃ lokamātaram
atrāpy udāharantīmam itihāsaṃ purātanam
vasiṣṭhasya ca saṃvādaṃ saudāsasya mahātmanaḥ
lokatraye muniśreṣṭha bhagavaṃl lokabhāvana
gāvo bhaviṣyaṃ bhūtaṃ ca goṣu yajñāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ
prātar namasyās tā gāvas tataḥ puṣṭim avāpnuyāt
lokān anantān samavāpya dhīraḥ
pramodate sukṛtī svargaloke
saurabhyaḥ puṇyakarmāṇaḥ pāvakāḥ śubhadarśanāḥ
yadarthaṃ gośatāś caiva saurabhyaḥ surasattama
tac ca me śṛṇu kārtsnyena vadato hy arisūdana
purā devayuge tāta daityendreṣu mahātmasu
tāḥ kila śṛṅgam ākāṅkṣan varṣātapanivāraṇam
mānuṣīṃ ca tvacaṃ gurvīṃ śītavātātapāsahām
vastrāvaraṇagocarmam avirodhaḥ parasparam
tato brahmā dadau tāsāṃ manasā dhāritāṃ parām
āhūya manujaṃ prāha goṣu svāṃ tanum ādadhaḥ
ācchādanaṃ cāvaraṇaṃ yūyaṃ gṛhṇīta mānavāḥ
śītavātātapaṃ yena dhārayanti prajārthinaḥ
sukumāratvacaṃ yūyaṃ vastrair ācchādayiṣyatha
evam astv iti tāḥ prādāt tvacaṃ svāṃ carma eva ca
phalāni ca rasāḍhyāni mṛdūni rucirāṇi ca
apsarobhiś ca sahito bhāti vai goprado naraḥ
yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
surāṇām asurāṇāṃ ca bhūtānāṃ ca pitāmaha
prabhuḥ sraṣṭā ca bhagavān munibhiḥ stūyate vibhuḥ
tasyopari kathaṃ hy eṣa golokaḥ sthānatāṃ gataḥ
saṃśayo me mahān eṣa tan me vyākhyātum arhasi
bhīṣmaḥ
manovāgbuddhayas tāvad ekasthāḥ kurusattama
tato me śṛṇu kārtsnyena gomahābhāgyam uttamam
puṇyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ tathā svastyayanaṃ mahat
kīrtir viharatāṃ loke gavāṃ yogeṣu bhaktimān
śrūyate hi purāṇeṣu maharṣīṇāṃ mahātmanām
saṃsthāne sarvalokānāṃ devānāṃ cāpi saṃbhave
devatārthe 'mṛtārthe ca yajñārthe caiva bhārata
surabhir nāma vikhyātā rohiṇī kāmarūpiṇī
saṃkalpya manasā pūrvaṃ rohiṇī hy amṛtānanā
ghoraṃ tapaḥ samāsthāya nirmitā viśvakarmaṇā
puruṣaṃ cāsṛjad bhūyas tejasā tapasā ca ha
dedīpyamānaṃ vapuṣā samiddham iva pāvakam
so 'paśyad divyarūpāṃ tāṃ surabhiṃ rohiṇīṃ tadā
dṛṣṭvaiva cātivimanāḥ so 'bhavat kāmamohitaḥ
taṃ kāmārtam atho jñātvā svayaṃbhūr lokabhāvanaḥ
mārto bhava tathā caiṣa bhagavān abhyabhāṣata
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃs tatra mārtāṇḍa iti viśrutaḥ
cakāra nāma taṃ dṛṣṭvā tasyārtībhāvam uttamam
so 'dadād bhagavāṃs tasmai mārtāṇḍāya mahātmane
surūpāṃ surabhiṃ kanyāṃ tapas tejomayīṃ śubhām
yathā mayaiṣa codbhūtas tvaṃ caiveṣā ca rohiṇī
maithunaṃ gatavantau ca tathā cotpatsyati prajā
prajā bhaviṣyate puṇyā pavitraṃ paramaṃ ca vām
na cāpy agamyāgamanād doṣaṃ prāpsyasi karhi cit
tvatprajāsaṃbhavaṃ kṣīraṃ bhaviṣyati paraṃ haviḥ
yajñeṣu cājyabhāgānāṃ tvatprajāmūlajo vidhiḥ
prajāśuśrūṣavaś caiva ye bhaviṣyanti rohiṇi
tava tenaiva puṇyena golokaṃ yāntu mānavāḥ
idaṃ pavitraṃ paramam ṛṣabhaṃ nāma karhi cit
yad vai jñātvā dvijā loke mokṣyante yonisaṃkarāt
etatkriyāḥ prapatsyante mantrabrāhmaṇasaṃskṛtāḥ
devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca havyakavyapurogamāḥ
tata etena puṇyena prajās tava tu rohiṇi
ūrdhvaṃ mamāpi lokasya vatsyante nirupadravāḥ
bhadraṃ tebhyaś ca bhadraṃ te ye prajāsu bhavanti vai
yugaṃdharāś ca te putrāḥ santu lokasya dhāraṇe
yān yān kāmayase lokāṃs tāṃl lokān anuyāsyasi
sarvadevagaṇāś caiva tava yāsyanti putratām
tava stanasamudbhūtaṃ pibanto 'mṛtam uttamam
evam etān varān sarvān agṛhṇāt surabhis tadā
bruvataḥ sarvalokeśān nirvṛtiṃ cāgamat parām
sṛṣṭvā prajāś ca vipulā lokasaṃdhāraṇāya vai
brahmaṇā samanujñātā surabhir lokam āviśat
evaṃ varapradānena svayaṃbhor eva bhārata
upariṣṭād gavāṃ lokaṃ proktaṃ te sarvam āditaḥ
yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
surabheḥ kāḥ prajāḥ pūrvaṃ mārtāṇḍād abhavan purā
bhīṣmaḥ
etan me śaṃsa tattvena goṣu me prīyate manaḥ
śṛṇu nāmāni divyāni gomātṝṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ
yābhir vyāptās trayo lokāḥ kalyāṇābhir janādhipa
surabhyaḥ prathamodbhūtā yāś ca syuḥ prathamāḥ prajāḥ
mayocyamānāḥ śṛṇu tāḥ prāpsyase vipulaṃ yaśaḥ
taptvā tapo ghoratapāḥ surabhir dīptatejasī
suṣāvaikādaśa sutān rudrā ye chandasi stutāḥ
ajaikapād ahirbudhnyas tryambakaś ca mahāyaśāḥ
vṛṣākapiś ca śaṃbhuś ca kapālī raivatas tathā
haraś ca bahurūpaś ca ugra ugro 'tha vīryavān
tasya caivātmajaḥ śrīmān viśvarūpo mahāyaśāḥ
ekādaśaite kathitā rudrās te nāma nāmataḥ
mahātmāno mahāyogās tejoyuktā mahābalāḥ
ete variṣṭhajanmāno devānāṃ brahmavādinām
viprāṇāṃ prakṛtir loke eta eva hi viśrutāḥ
eta ekādaśa proktā rudrās tribhuvaneśvarāḥ
śataṃ tv etat samākhyātaṃ śatarudraṃ mahātmanām
suṣuve prathamāṃ kanyāṃ surabhiḥ pṛthivīṃ tadā
viśvakāmadughā dhenur yā dhārayati dehinaḥ
sutaṃ gobrāhmaṇaṃ rājann ekam ity abhidhīyate
gobrāhmaṇasya jananī surabhiḥ parikīrtyate
sṛṣṭvā tu prathamaṃ rudrān varadān brahmasaṃbhavān
paścāt prabhuṃ grahapatiṃ suṣuve lokasaṃmatam
somarājānam amṛtaṃ yajñasarvasvam uttamam
oṣadhīnāṃ rasānāṃ ca devānāṃ jīvitasya ca
tataḥ śriyaṃ ca medhāṃ ca kīrtiṃ devīṃ sarasvatīm
catasraḥ suṣuve kanyā yogeṣu niyatāḥ sthitāḥ
etāḥ sṛṣṭvā prajā eṣā surabhiḥ kāmarūpiṇī
suṣuve caramaṃ bhūyo divyā gomātaraḥ śubhāḥ
puṇyāṃ māyāṃ madhuścyotāṃ śivāṃ śīghrāṃ saridvarām
hiraṇyavarṇāṃ subhagāṃ gavyāṃ pṛśnīṃ kuthāvatīm
aṅgāvatīṃ ghṛtavatīṃ dadhikṣīrapayovatīm
amoghāṃ surasāṃ satyāṃ revatīṃ mārutīṃ rasām
ajāṃ ca sikatāṃ caiva śuddhadhūmām adhāriṇīm
jīvāṃ prāṇavatīṃ dhanyāṃ śuddhāṃ dhenuṃ dhanāvahām
indrām ṛddhiṃ ca śāntiṃ ca śāntapāpāṃ saridvarām
catvāriṃśatam ekāṃ ca dhanyās tā divi pūjitāḥ
bhūyo jajñe surabhyāś ca śrīmāṃś candrāṃśusaprabhaḥ
vṛṣo dakṣa iti khyātaḥ kaṇṭhe maṇitalaprabhaḥ
sragvī kakudmān dyutimān mṛṇālasadṛśaprabhaḥ
surabhyanumate datto dhvajo māheśvaras tu saḥ
surabhyaḥ kāmarūpiṇyo gāvaḥ puṇyārtham uttamāḥ
ādityebhyo vasubhyaś ca viśvebhyaś ca dadur varān
surabhis tu tapas taptvā suṣuve gās tataḥ punaḥ
yā dattā lokapālānām indrādīnāṃ yudhiṣṭhira
suṣṭutāṃ kapilāṃ caiva rohiṇīṃ ca yaśasvinīm
sarvakāmadughāṃ caiva marutāṃ kāmarūpiṇīm
gāvo mṛṣṭadughā hy etāś catasro 'mṛtasaṃskṛtāḥ
yāsāṃ bhūtvā purā vatsāḥ pibanty amṛtam uttamam
suṣṭutāṃ devarājāya vāsavāya mahātmane
kapilāṃ dharmarājāya varuṇāya ca rohiṇīm
sarvakāmadughāṃ dhenuṃ rājñe vaiśravaṇāya ca
ity etā lokam ahitā viśrutāḥ surabheḥ prajāḥ
etāsāṃ prajayā pūrṇā pṛthivī munipuṃgava
gobhyaḥ prabhavate sarvaṃ yat kiṃ cid iha śobhanam
surabhyapatyam ity etan nāmatas te 'nupūrvaśaḥ
yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
kīrtitaṃ brūhi rājendra kiṃ bhūyaḥ kathayāmi te
surabhyās tu tadā devyāḥ kīrtir lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī
medhā ca pravarā devī yāś catasro 'bhiviśrutāḥ
pṛthag gobhyaḥ kim etāḥ syur utāho goṣu saṃśritāḥ
bhīṣmaḥ
devāḥ ke vāśritā goṣu tan me brūhi pitāmaha
yaṃ devaṃ saṃśritā gāvas taṃ devaṃ devasaṃjñitam
yad yad devāśritaṃ daivaṃ tat tad daivaṃ dvijā viduḥ
sarveṣām eva devānāṃ pūrvaṃ kila samudbhave
indraḥ
amṛtārthe surapatiḥ surabhiṃ samupasthitaḥ
iccheyam amṛtaṃ dattaṃ tvayā devi rasādhikam
tvatprasādāc chivaṃ mahyam amaratvaṃ bhaved iti
surabhiḥ
vatso bhūtvā surapate pibasva prasnavaṃ mama
tato 'maratvam api tat sthānam aindram avāpsyasi
na ca te vṛtrahan yuddhe vyathāribhyo bhaviṣyati
balārtham ātmanaḥ śakra prasnavaṃ piba me vibho
bhīṣmaḥ
tato 'dhayat stanaṃ tasyāḥ surabhyāḥ surasattamaḥ
amaratvaṃ surūpatvaṃ balaṃ ca padam uttamam
puraṃdaro 'mṛtaṃ pītvā prahṛṣṭaḥ samupasthitaḥ
putro 'haṃ tava bhadraṃ te brūhi kiṃ karavāṇi te
surabhiḥ
kṛtaṃ putra tvayā sarvam upayāhi triviṣṭapam
pālayasva surān sarvāñ jahi ye suraśatravaḥ
na ca gobrāhmaṇe 'vajñā kāryā te śāntim icchatā
gobrāhmaṇasya niḥśvāsaḥ śoṣayed api devatāḥ
gobrāhmaṇapriyo nityaṃ svastiśabdam udāharan
pṛthivyām antarikṣe ca nākapṛṣṭhe ca vikramet
yac ca te 'nyad bhavet kṛtyaṃ tan me brūyāḥ samāsataḥ
tat te sarvaṃ kariṣyāmi satyenaitad bravīmi te
indraḥ
iccheyaṃ goṣu niyataṃ vastuṃ devi bravīmi te
surabhiḥ
ebhiḥ suragaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ mamānugraham ācara
gavāṃ śarīraṃ pratyakṣam etat kauśika lakṣaye
yo yatrotsahate vastuṃ sa tatra vasatāṃ suraḥ
sarvaṃ pavitraṃ paramaṃ gavāṃ gātraṃ supūjitam
bhīṣmaḥ
tathā kuruṣva bhadraṃ te yathā tvaṃ śakra manyase
tasyās tad vacanaṃ śrutvā surabhyāḥ surasattamaḥ
saha sarvaiḥ suragaṇair abhajat saurabhīṃ prajām
śṛṅge vaktre ca jihvāyāṃ devarājaḥ samāviśat
sarvacchidreṣu pavanaḥ pādeṣu marutāṃ gaṇāḥ
kakudaṃ sarvago rudraḥ kukṣau caiva hutāśanaḥ
sarasvatī staneṣv agryā śrīḥ purīṣe jagatpriyā
mūtre kīrtiḥ sumahatī medhā payasi śāśvatī
vaktre somaś ca vai devo hṛdaye bhagavān yamaḥ
dharmaḥ pucche kriyā lomni bhāskaraś cakṣuṣī śritaḥ
siddhāḥ saṃdhiṣu siddhiś ca tapas tejaś ca ceṣṭane
evaṃ sarve suragaṇā niyatā gātravartmasu
mahatī devatā gāvo brāhmaṇaiḥ parisaṃskṛtāḥ
gām āśrayanti sahitā devā hi prabhaviṣṇavaḥ
kim anyat sarvakāmena na vidadhyād bhavān priyam
bhāvataḥ parayā bhaktyā pūjayasva nareśvara
gāvas tu paramaṃ loke pavitraṃ pāvanaṃ haviḥ
nipātya bhakṣitaḥ svargād bhārgavaḥ phenapaḥ kila
sa ca prāṇān punar labdhvā tato golokam āśritaḥ
yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
kaḥ phenapeti nāmnāsau kathaṃ vā bhakṣitaḥ purā
mṛta ujjīvitaḥ kasmāt kathaṃ golokam āśritaḥ
viruddhe mānuṣe loke tathā samayavartmasu
ṛte daivaṃ hi duṣprāpaṃ mānuṣeṣu viśeṣataḥ
bhīṣmaḥ
saṃśayo me mahān atra tan me vyākhyātum arhasi
śrūyate bhārgave vaṃśe sumitro nāma bhārata
vedādhyayanasaṃpanno vipule tapasi sthitaḥ
vānaprasthāśrame yuktaḥ svakarmanirataḥ sadā
vinayācāratattvajñaḥ sarvadharmārthakovidaḥ
yattas triṣavaṇasnāyī saṃdhyopāsanatatparaḥ
agnihotrarataḥ kṣānto japañ juhvac ca nityadā
pitṝn devāṃś ca niyatam atithīṃś caiva pūjayan
prāṇasaṃdhāraṇārthaṃ ca yat kiṃ cid upahārayan
giris triśikharo nāma yataḥ prabhavate nadī
kulaheti purāṇeṣu viśrutā rudranirmitā
tasyās tīre same deśe puṣpamālāsamākule
vanyauṣadhidrumopete nānāpakṣimṛgāyute
vyapetadaṃśamaśake dhvāṅkṣagṛdhrair asevite
kṛṣṇadarbhatṛṇaprāye suramye jyotiraśmini
sarvonnataiḥ samaiḥ śyāmair yājñīyais tarubhir vṛte
tatrāśramapadaṃ puṇyaṃ bhṛgūṇām abhavat purā
uvāsa tatra niyataḥ sumitro nāma bhārgavaḥ
yathoddiṣṭena pūrveṣāṃ bhṛgūṇāṃ sādhuvartmanām
tasmā āṅgirasaḥ kaś cid dadau gāṃ śarkarīṃ śubhām
varṣāsu paścime māsi paurṇamāsyāṃ śucivrataḥ
sa tāṃ labdhvā dharmaśīlaś cintayām āsa tatparaḥ
sumitraḥ parayā bhaktyā jananīm iva mātaram
tena saṃdhukṣyamāṇā sā rohiṇī kāmacāriṇī
vivṛddhim agamac chreṣṭhā prāṇataś ca sudarśanā
sirāvimuktapārśvāntā vipulāṃ kāntim udvahat
śyāmapārśvāntapṛṣṭhāntā surabhir madhupiṅgalā
bṛhatī sūkṣmaromāntā rūpodagrā tanutvacā
kṛṣṇapucchā śvetavaktrā samavṛttapayodharā
pṛṣṭhonnatā pūrvanatā śaṅkukarṇī sulocanā
dīrghajihvā hrasvaśṛṅgī saṃpūrṇadaśanāntarā
māṃsādhikagalāntā sā prasannā śubhadarśanā
nityaṃ śamayutā snigdhā saṃpūrṇodāttanisvanā
prājāpatyair gavāṃ nityaṃ praśastair lakṣaṇair yutā
yauvanastheva vanitā śuśubhe rūpaśobhayā
vṛṣeṇopagatā sā tu kalyā madhuradarśanā
mithunaṃ janayām āsa tulyarūpam ivātmanaḥ
saṃvardhayām āsa sa tāṃ savatsāṃ bhārgavo ṛṣiḥ
tayoḥ prajādhisaṃsargāt sahasraṃ ca gavām abhūt
gavāṃ jātisahasrāṇi saṃbhūtāni parasparam
ṛṣabhāṇāṃ ca rājendra naivāntaḥ pratidṛśyate
tair āśramapadaṃ ramyam araṇyaṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ
samākulaṃ samabhavan meghair iva nabhaḥsthalam
kāni cit padmavarṇāni kiṃśukābhāni kāni cit
rukmavarṇāni cānyāni candrāṃśusadṛśāni ca
tathā rājatavarṇāni kāni cil lohitāni vai
nīlalohitatāmrāṇi kṛṣṇāni kapilāni ca
nānārāgavicitrāṇi yūthāni bahulāni ca
na ca kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ snehād vatsānām upajīvati
bhārgavaḥ kevalaṃ cāsīd gavāṃ prāṇāyane rataḥ
tathā śuśrūṣatas tasya gavāṃ hitam avekṣataḥ
vyatīyāt sumahān kālo vatsocchiṣṭena vartataḥ
kṣutpipāsāpariśrāntaḥ satataṃ prasnavaṃ gavām
vatsair ucchiṣṭamuditaṃ bahukṣīratayā bahu
pītavāṃs tena nāmāsya phenapety abhiviśrutam
bhīṣmaḥ
gautamasyābhiniṣpannam evaṃ nāma yudhiṣṭhira
kadā cit kāmarūpiṇyo gāvaḥ strīveṣam āsthitāḥ
hrade krīḍanti saṃhṛṣṭā gāyantyaḥ puṇyalakṣaṇāḥ
dadṛśus tasya gāvo vai vismayotphullalocanāḥ
striyaḥ
ūcuś ca kā yūyam iti striyo mānuṣayā girā
gāva eva vayaṃ sarvāḥ karmabhiḥ śobhanair yutāḥ
sarvāḥ strīveṣadhāriṇyo yathākāmaṃ carāmahe
gāvaḥ
gavāṃ gāvaḥ paraṃ daivaṃ gavāṃ gāvaḥ parā gatiḥ
striyaḥ
kathayadhvam ihāsmākaṃ kena vaḥ sukṛtāṃ gatiḥ
asmākaṃ haviṣā devā brāhmaṇās tarpitās tathā
kavyena pitaraś caiva havyenāgniś ca tarpitaḥ
prajayā ca tathāsmākaṃ kṛṣir abhyuddhṛtā sadā
śakaṭaiś cāpi saṃyuktā daśavāhaśatena vai
tad etaiḥ sukṛtaiḥ sphītair vayaṃ yāś caiva naḥ prajāḥ
golokam anusaṃprāptā yaḥ paraḥ kāmagocaraḥ
yūyaṃ tu sarvā rohiṇyaḥ saprajāḥ sahapuṃgavāḥ
gāvaḥ
adhogāminya ity eva paśyāmo divyacakṣuṣā
evaṃ gavāṃ paraṃ daivaṃ gāva eva parāyaṇam
svapakṣyās tāraṇīyā vaḥ śaraṇāya gatā vayam
kim asmābhiḥ karaṇīyaṃ vartitavyaṃ kathaṃ cana
striyaḥ
prāpnuyāma ca golokaṃ bhavāma na ca garhitāḥ
vartate rantidevasya satraṃ varṣasahasrakam
tatra tasya nṛpasyāśu paśutvam upagacchata
tatas tasyopayogena paśutve yajñasaṃskṛtāḥ
bhīṣmaḥ
golokān prāpsyatha śubhāṃs tena puṇyena saṃyutāḥ
etat tāsāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā gavāṃ saṃhṛṣṭamānasāḥ
gamanāya manaś cakrur autsukyaṃ cāgaman param
na hi no bhārgavo dātā paśutvenopayojanam
yajñas tasya narendrasya vartate dharmatas tathā
vayaṃ na cānanujñātāḥ śaktā gantuṃ kathaṃ cana
avocann atha tatratyā bhārgavo vadhyatām ayam
etat sarvā rocayata na hi śakyam ato 'nyathā
lokān prāptuṃ sahāsmābhir niścayaḥ kriyatām ayam
na tu tāsāṃ sametānāṃ kā cid ghoreṇa cakṣuṣā
śaknoti bhārgavaṃ draṣṭuṃ satkṛtenopasaṃyutā
atha padmasavarṇābhā bhāskarāṃśusamaprabhāḥ
japālohitatāmrākṣyo nirmāṃsakaṭhinānanāḥ
rohiṇyaḥ kapilāḥ prāhuḥ sarvāsāṃ vai samakṣataḥ
meghastanitanirghoṣās tejobhir abhirañjitāḥ
vayaṃ hi taṃ vadhiṣyāmaḥ sumitraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ
gāvaḥ
sukṛtaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā kiṃ naḥ śreyo vidhāsyatha
kapilāḥ sarvavarṇeṣu pradhānatvam avāpsyatha
bhīṣmaḥ
gavāṃ śataphalāc caikāṃ dattvā phalam avāpsyati
etad gavāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā kapilā hṛṣṭamānasāḥ
bhīṣmaḥ
cakruḥ sarvā bhārgavasya sumitrasya vadhe matim
yās tu gomātaras tasya kāmacāriṇya āgatāḥ
samīpaṃ hi sumitrasya kṛtajñāḥ samupasthitāḥ
abhipraśasya caivāhus tam ṛṣiṃ puṇyadarśanāḥ
golokād āgatā veda ṛṣe gomātaro vayam
suprītāḥ sma varaṃ gṛhṇa yam icchasi mahāmune
sumitraḥ
